# UA-2c — the group kinds: MuSig2 (BIP-327, bn254 port) and FROST (RFC 9591 secp256k1, bn254), stored keys, `groups.json` — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** the third of three UA-2 sub-plans ([roadmap](2026-10-12-ua-roadmap.md); owner 2026-10-12): the group kinds
`musig2-secp256k1` 0x0110, `frost-secp256k1` 0x0111, `musig2-bn254` 0x0112, `frost-bn254` 0x0113 end to end — the registry's
stored-key path (`keyPtr` via SSTORE2; `auth` carries only the signature), registration checks in each kind's verifier
(BIP-327 KeyAgg with `ecrecover` hints / the bn254 port with ECMUL; FROST DKG PoKs and `Y = Σ C_i0`), the verifiers on the
stored key, `groups.json` from Rust signers in `scaas-ua` (BIP-327, ZF `frost-secp256k1`, the bn254 `frost-core`
ciphersuite), a noble cross-check of the secp256k1 rows, T-FROST, and the binding crates' lockfiles refreshed. Not here:
JS/WASM signers and auth builders (UA F3), deployment (UA-3).

**Architecture:** For a stored kind `register` validates key data (`UAKinds`), calls the kind's `verifyPop(keyData,
popMsg, pop)` — the whole group check plus one group signature — and stores `vk = keyData[0:33]` (secp256k1) or `[0:64]`
(bn254) with SSTORE2; `requireAuthorized` requires empty `auth.keyData`, reads `vk` and calls `verify(vk, ua_msg, sig)`
(capped, fail closed, as UA-2a). MuSig2 secp256k1 signs BIP-340 by `x(Q)` (`library Bip340`, extracted from
`Bip340Verifier`); MuSig2 bn254 and FROST bn254 sign scaas Schnorr (`CurveSigs`); FROST secp256k1 signs RFC 9591 and is
never verified as BIP-340. `groups.json` comes from one deterministic generator; Rust re-checks every row with the registry's
rules, noble re-checks the secp256k1 rows independently, and forge runs every row through the real verifiers and registry.

**Tech Stack:** Solidity ^0.8.20, Foundry (forge 1.3.5, osaka), EIP-196/197 bn254, `ecrecover`, SHA-256, `MODEXP`;
Rust 1.90 — `scaas-ua` default build gains `src/groups.rs` (registry-side checks; k256 `hash2curve`); feature `signers`:
`frost-core =3.0.0`, `frost-secp256k1 =3.0.0`, `rand_chacha =0.3.1`; Node 22 with the existing `@noble/*`.

**Spec:** `docs/contracts/accounts.md` §1.6 rows 0x0110–0x0113, §1.6.1, CTR-UA-17, §1.8 steps 6 and 8, §1.9, §1.15, §1.16
(`groups.json`, T-FROST).

**Base:** `main` at the commit that adds this plan (parent `e26749a`; nothing under `smartcontracts/` or `clients/` changed
since `3eb2e2b`). Tasks 1–5 were built as spikes in scratch copies on 2026-10-12 and run green: Rust all suites + `signers`
6 passed / 1 ignored, clippy clean; node 4/4; FROST 17 tests; registry/MuSig2 suites 1 + 11 + 13 + 32; full forge (dry-run of
Tasks 1–6 on a clean copy) 1,051 passed / 10 skipped. Tasks 6–7 were written by the controller. Not run in scratch: the merged
tree, CI.

## Global Constraints

- Paths relative to `/home/ha/work/ainative/scaas`; worktree `.claude/worktrees/ua2c` on branch `ua2c-groups` from `main`.
  Heavy commands (forge, cargo, node tests) only through `scripts/capped`, one at a time; `/usr/bin/git` and `/usr/bin/grep`;
  forge in `smartcontracts/evm`.
- Task order is fixed: 1 vectors + signers → 2 SSTORE2 + registry stored kinds → 3 FROST verifiers → 4 MuSig2 verifiers +
  `library Bip340` (imports Task 3's `FrostSecp256k1.hashToScalar` / `DST_CHAL` in its test fixtures) → 5 registry on
  `groups.json` → 6 binding lockfiles → 7 docs.
- `groups.json` is generated only by the `scaas-ua` generator (feature `signers`); a Rust test checks byte-for-byte
  regeneration. `kinds.json`, `structures.json`, `signatures.json`, `ed25519_fuzz.json` are not modified.
- Stored kinds: `auth = abi.encode(bytes keyData, bytes sig)` with `keyData` empty, else `UAUnauthorized`.
- Every verifier returns `false` and never reverts on malformed input; FROST signatures are never accepted as BIP-340 and
  vice versa (T-FROST).
- No size optimisation work (owner: wait for Glamsterdam); the size gate must pass.
- Commit trailer: the model that wrote the commit, e.g. `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Never
  `git add` `claude-code-api.txt`, `out/`, `cache/`, `target/`, `node_modules/`, `smartcontracts/evm/lib/`.

## Review Focus

- **Rogue keys and fake groups:** a MuSig2 key list whose KeyAgg does not give `Q` (wrong hint, wrong parity, unsorted,
  second-key rule, `x(P_i) ≥ N`), a FROST transcript with a bad PoK, `Y ≠ Σ C_i0`, a participant naming its own key as `Y`,
  wrong degree — all refused at registration, with the error `groups.json` names.
- **Stored-key path:** `keyPtr` content equals `vk`; a non-empty `auth.keyData` for a stored kind is refused; generation 2
  hands gen-1 stored records to gen 1; an msig member of a stored kind reads its own record's `vk`.
- **Cross-scheme confusion:** a FROST(secp256k1) signature never passes `Bip340Verifier` / the MuSig2 verifier and a BIP-340
  signature never passes the FROST verifier; the group key blocks the same keyHash group as `bip340` / `schnorr-bn254`.
- **Gas at the extremes:** `register` at n = 16 and t = n = 16 stays far under `verifyBudget`; `verify` under its cap cold;
  a starved `requireAuthorized` gives `UAVerifyGas` (a starved group registration gives `UAPoP`, C13).
- **Malformed transcripts:** truncated key data, header bounds (t = n = 17, t > n), non-canonical or off-curve points,
  identity, prefixes other than 02/03 — key validation or the verifier refuses, never a revert from a precompile.

## Rulings (docs vs code) and defaults taken

- **C1** Stored kinds: `vk = keyData[0:33]` / `[0:64]` stored with SSTORE2 (`src/common/SSTORE2.sol`: neither the repo nor
  OpenZeppelin has one as a library); `requireAuthorized` requires empty `auth.keyData` and verifies against `keyPtr`.
- **C2** The group check lives in the kind's `verifyPop(keyData, msg, pop)`; `verify(vk, msg, sig)` checks the stored key;
  MuSig2-secp256k1 `pop = sig (64) ‖ n × H_i (64)` with `H_i = a_i·P_i`.
- **C3** Signers and vectors in Rust (`scaas-ua` feature `signers`); JS/WASM signers and auth builders deferred to UA F3; the
  node cross-check covers the secp256k1 rows only (the bn254 ports are single-implementation).
- **C5** FROST `verifyPop` does not re-check `C_ik` (k ≥ 1): they enter no equation on chain and key validation already
  checks their encoding (saves ~1.2 M at n = 16).
- **C6/C16** bn254 style as built: `Musig2Bn254Verifier` uses `UASafeVerifier` with a `_checkGas(bool pop)` hook (`POP_GAS`
  230 k); `FrostBn254Verifier` has no wrapper (all points checked in Solidity before any precompile). Both tested.
- **C7/C14** Point sums in Jacobian coordinates (no inversion), `Q` compared projectively (x and parity); a degenerate
  partial sum (doubling / cancellation to infinity) refuses the transcript (p ≈ 2^-128 for honest groups).
- **C8** DKG PoK challenge = frost-core 3.0.0: `H_dkg(id (32-byte BE) ‖ C_i0 ‖ R_i)`, DST `contextString ‖ "dkg"` (48 bytes
  mod `N` for secp256k1; the keccak widening mod `r` for bn254).
- **C9** MuSig2 implemented once (generic, over k256 and arkworks) instead of the `musig2` crate, so the bn254 port shares
  the code the BIP-327 vectors test; no tweaks.
- **C10** bn254 port gaps settled (Task 7 writes them into §1.6.1): `L` a plain 32-byte tagged hash; scalar hashes widened to
  512 bits mod `r`; FROST(bn254) `H4`/`H5` output the scalar's 32 bytes, no `HID`.
- **C11** Registry-side group checks in `scaas-ua` `src/groups.rs` (default build, reached through `verify::verify`); signers
  behind `signers`.
- **C12** The binding crates' `Cargo.lock` (stale since UA-2a; their `--locked` CI fails on main) are refreshed minimally in
  Task 6, not deferred to the dirs rename.
- **C13** The group PoP call is capped at `verifyBudget` without the caller-holds-that-much pre-check (else every group
  registration needs a ≥ 12.4 M limit; Hedera bills 80 % of the limit): a starved group registration reverts `UAPoP`, no
  record is created.
- **C15** msig members of stored kinds are allowed (existing member path; `vk` from the member's record).
- **C17** FROST secp256k1 keeps its own point decompression (Task 4 imports Task 3's hash helpers, so Task 3 precedes the
  `Bip340` extraction; ~10 duplicated lines accepted).
- **C18** §1.8's `checkGroup` wording becomes `verifyPop`; §1.6.1 "affine additions" becomes "point additions (Jacobian, no
  inversion)" (Task 7).
- **C19** The `rfc9591-e5-as-bip340` T-FROST row signs a 4-byte message and is skipped by the forge test (`Bip340Verifier`
  takes 32-byte messages only); the row is invalid anyway and the other 9 run.

## Open questions (not blocking)

- `UAKinds.validateKeyData` dominates registration at t = n = 16 on secp256k1 (1.6 M, 273 point checks) — the registry
  could skip point checks the verifier repeats; left as is (no gas optimisation phase).

The UA client dirs rename (owner, option a), UA-3 (deployment, Zether kinds), UA F3 (JS/WASM signers, stored-kind auth

#### groups.json schema (version 1) — what the verifier and registry tasks consume

##### Top level

| Key | Content |
|---|---|
| `note`, `version` (1) | provenance; single-implementation caveat for the bn254 kinds |
| `context` / `contextOther` | `"A"` / `"B"` |
| `contexts.A`, `contexts.B` | `{chainId, chainUid, registry}` — copied from signatures.json (A: 31337 / `0x5ca0…01`; B: 7622) |
| `digest` | the fixed digest the `sigs` rows sign (= signatures.json `digest`) |
| `ciphersuites` | prose summary of the hash definitions (below) |
| `groups[]` | 20 registered groups (4 kinds; `role` `main` or `gas`) |
| `bip327` | official BIP-327 vectors + KeyAgg rows with Q/coefficients/hints |
| `rfc9591E5` | RFC 9591 Appendix E.5 FROST(secp256k1, SHA-256) |
| `zfDkg` | the ZF frost-secp256k1 3.0.0 DKG vector in registry key-data layout (PoK check only, no PoP) |
| `tFrost[]` | T-FROST cross-scheme rows |

##### `groups[]`

| `kind` | groups (`role`) |
|---|---|
| 272 `musig2-secp256k1` | n = 3 (main), 2, 8, 16 (gas) |
| 273 `frost-secp256k1` | (t, n) = (2, 3) (main), (2, 2), (2, 8), (8, 8), (2, 16), (16, 16) (gas) |
| 274 `musig2-bn254` | n = 3 (main), 2, 8, 16 |
| 275 `frost-bn254` | (t, n) = (2, 3) (main), (2, 2), (2, 8), (8, 8), (2, 16), (16, 16) |

Fields (all always present; empty `[]` / `"0x"` where not applicable):

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `name`, `label`, `role`, `kind`, `kindName`, `n`, `t` | MuSig2: `t = n` |
| `secrets` | MuSig2: member secret keys in member order; FROST: signing shares of participants 1..n |
| `groupSecret` | MuSig2: `d = Σ a_i·sk_i` (`Q = d·G`, before any BIP-340 negation); FROST: `s = Σ a_i0` (`Y = s·G`) |
| `members` | MuSig2: `pk_i` in key-data order (33 B SEC1 compressed / 64 B bn254) |
| `L`, `coefficients` | MuSig2: `L` and `a_i` (32 B big-endian) per member |
| `hints` | musig2-secp256k1: `H_i = a_i·P_i`, 64 B `x ‖ y` each (uncompressed, no prefix) |
| `dkg[]` | FROST: per participant `{id, commitments[t], R, mu, preimage, challenge}` (`preimage` = the `H_dkg` input, `challenge` = `c_i`) |
| `keyData`, `ua`, `keyHash`, `vk` | `ua = ua_of(kind, keyData)`; `vk = keyData[0:33]` (secp256k1 kinds) / `[0:64]` (bn254 kinds) — what SSTORE2 stores |
| `pop` | `{context: "A", popDigest, message, sig, blob}` (+ `signers` for FROST): `message = uaMessage(kind, PURPOSE_POP, popDigest(A, ua, authRef 0))`; `blob` = the `register` / `verifyPop` `pop` argument; `sig` = its signature part |
| `sigs[]` | main only: `{purpose ∈ metatx, member; purposeTag, digest, message, sig}` (+ `signers` for FROST): `verify(vk, message, sig)` = true |
| `negatives[]` | main only, see below |

`pop.blob` layouts: musig2-secp256k1 `sig (64, BIP-340) ‖ n × H_i (64)`; frost-secp256k1 `R (33) ‖ z (32)`;
musig2-bn254 and frost-bn254 `R (64) ‖ z (32)` (scaas Schnorr). For FROST, `pop.sig == pop.blob`.

### `negatives[]`

| `entry` | Fields | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| `register` | `name, error, keyData, ua, popDigest, message, pop, sigValid, comment` | `register(kind, keyData, pop, 0, "")` under context A reverts with `error`: `UAKeyData` (UAKinds.validateKeyData refuses) or `UAPoP` (key data valid, `verifyPop(keyData, message, pop)` = false). `sigValid` = the signature part of `pop` alone verifies against keyData's Q/Y over `message` (true ⇒ only the group check refuses it) |
| `verify` | `name, error ("false"), vk, message, sig, comment` | `verify(vk, message, sig)` = false |

Names: musig2-* `wrong-q-own-key` (T-REG-6 attack), `wrong-q-negated`, `unsorted-keys`, `duplicate-key`, `n-one`,
`n-seventeen`, `pop-sig-mutated`, `pop-other-context`; secp256k1 only `bad-hint` (H_1 = P_1), `hints-shifted`
(H_1 + G, H_2 − G: Σ unchanged), `hint-missing`, `hint-extra`; bn254 only `pop-trailing-byte`. frost-* `bad-pok`,
`pok-ids-swapped`, `commitments-extra`, `commitments-missing`, `t-one`, `t-gt-n`, `y-not-sum`, `rogue-contribution`,
`y-negated`, `pop-sig-mutated`, `pop-other-context`; secp256k1 only `pop-bip340`. Verify side (all kinds):
`wrong-purpose`, `sig-mutated`, `s-equals-order` / `z-equals-order`; frost-secp256k1 `r-prefix-04`. Counts: 15 / 16 / 12 / 14 = 57.

##### Hash definitions (pinned by the vectors)

**musig2-secp256k1** = BIP-327 KeyAgg: `L = tagged("KeyAgg list", pk_1 ‖ … ‖ pk_n)` (33-byte keys),
`a_i = int(tagged("KeyAgg coefficient", L ‖ pk_i)) mod N`, `a_i = 1` for every `pk_i` equal to the first key that differs
from `pk_1`; `Q = Σ a_i·P_i` (keyData stores Q compressed with its parity). Hint check (EVM): `x(P_i) < N`,
`address(H_i) == ecrecover(0, 27 + (P_i[0] − 2), x(P_i), a_i·x(P_i) mod N)`; `Σ H_i == Q` (x and parity); BIP-340 by `x(Q)`.

**musig2-bn254** (our port): `L = tagged("KeyAgg list", P_1 ‖ … ‖ P_n)` — plain 32-byte SHA-256 tagged hash (a byte
string, not reduced), 64-byte points; `a_i = (tagged("KeyAgg coefficient", L ‖ P_i ‖ 0x00) ‖ tagged("KeyAgg coefficient", L ‖ P_i ‖ 0x01))`
as a 512-bit big-endian integer mod r; second-key rule as BIP-327; `Q = Σ a_i·P_i` (ECMUL/ECADD); then scaas Schnorr
(`schnorr-bn254`) by Q. Signing (off chain only): nonce coefficient `b` = the same widening of `tagged("MuSig/noncecoef", aggnonce(128) ‖ Q(64) ‖ msg)`,
nonces = widened `tagged("MuSig/nonce", …)` (BIP-327 NonceGen layout with 64-byte keys), no parity negation, challenge = scaas Schnorr over `R ‖ Q ‖ msg`.

**frost-secp256k1** (RFC 9591 §6.5, frost-core 3.0.0 DKG): `H(label, m) = hash_to_field(m)` with
`expand_message_xmd(SHA-256)`, DST = `"FROST-secp256k1-SHA256-v1" ‖ label`, 48 bytes, big-endian mod N.
- signature: `c = H("chal", R(33) ‖ Y(33) ‖ msg)`; accept iff `z < N`, R decompresses (02/03), `z·G == R + c·Y`.
- **DKG PoK** (frost-core `keys::dkg::challenge`): preimage = `id_i (32 B big-endian scalar, = i) ‖ C_i0 (33) ‖ R_i (33)`
  (98 bytes), `c_i = H("dkg", preimage)` (DST `"FROST-secp256k1-SHA256-v1dkg"`); accept iff `R_i == μ_i·G − c_i·C_i0`.
  `(R_i, μ_i)` is frost-core's `proof_of_knowledge` Signature; `μ_i = k + a_i0·c_i`. Participants are `i = 1..n` in key-data order.
- group: `Y == Σ_i C_i0` (compared as the 33-byte encoding, parity included).

**frost-bn254** (FROST(bn254, keccak256), `contextString = "FROST-BN254-KECCAK256-SCAAS-v1"`):
`Hx(label, m) = (keccak256(cs ‖ label ‖ m ‖ 0x00) ‖ keccak256(cs ‖ label ‖ m ‖ 0x01))` as 512-bit big-endian mod r for
labels `rho`, `nonce`, `dkg` (`msg`, `com`: the same scalar's 32 bytes); `H2` = scaas Schnorr challenge over
`R(64) ‖ Y(64) ‖ msg`. DKG PoK preimage = `id_i (32 B big-endian) ‖ C_i0 (64) ‖ R_i (64)` (160 bytes), `c_i = Hx("dkg", preimage)`;
`R_i == μ_i·G − c_i·C_i0`; `Y == Σ C_i0`; then `schnorr-bn254` verify by Y.

##### `bip327`

`source` (bitcoin/bips commit 927b6de…), `sha256` per vendored file, `official.{key_sort_vectors, key_agg_vectors,
nonce_gen_vectors, nonce_agg_vectors, sign_verify_vectors}` verbatim (upper-case hex, as published), and `keyAgg[]` — the 4
valid KeyAgg cases (incl. duplicate and unsorted lists, which the registry's key-data rules refuse but the KeyAgg routine
must compute): `{keys, L, secondKey, coefficients, Q (33, with parity), expectedX (official), hints}`. Tweak vectors are
not included (the registry has no tweaks).

##### `rfc9591E5`

`{source, groupPublicKey, groupSecretKey, message (0x74657374 = "test", 4 bytes — not a 32-byte ua_msg), participantList [1,3],
sig, full}`; `full` = every value of Appendix E.5 (shares, nonce randomness, commitments, binding factors, sig shares).

##### `zfDkg`

`{source, t: 2, n: 3, keyData, ua, dkg[]}`: frost-secp256k1 3.0.0 `tests/helpers/vectors_dkg.json` (authored by ZF, not by
this generator) laid out as registry key data; its PoKs and `Y = Σ C_i0` must pass the on-chain DKG check. No PoP signature.

##### `tFrost[]`

`{name, verifier ("frost-secp256k1" | "bip340"), key (33 / 32 B), message, sig, valid, comment}`, 10 rows: RFC E.5, the
frost-secp256k1 main PoP, a BIP-340 signature by the FROST group secret, the musig2-secp256k1 main PoP, BIP-340 official
vector 0 — each under its own verifier (`valid: true`) and re-encoded for the other (`valid: false`: FROST `R ‖ z` → BIP-340
`x(R) ‖ z`; BIP-340 `r ‖ s` → FROST `(02 ‖ r) ‖ s` under `02 ‖ x`).

diff --git a/clients/clientkeys/test/ua-groups.test.js b/clients/clientkeys/test/ua-groups.test.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..90e47b5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/clients/clientkeys/test/ua-groups.test.js
@@ -0,0 +1,176 @@
+// smartcontracts/vectors/ua/groups.json (UA-2c, generated by clients/scaas-ua tests/groups.rs from k256 / ZF frost-core):
+// the secp256k1 rows re-verified here with noble through checks written from accounts.md §1.6.1 / RFC 9591 / frost-core's
+// DKG, not from the generator: BIP-327 KeyAgg + hints + BIP-340 (musig2-secp256k1), RFC 9591 FROST(secp256k1, SHA-256)
+// verify + DKG PoKs + Y = Σ C_i0 (frost-secp256k1), every negative refused for its stated reason, the official BIP-327
+// KeyAgg rows, RFC 9591 E.5, the ZF DKG vector and T-FROST. musig2-bn254 and frost-bn254 have no second implementation
+// (single-implementation kinds, accounts.md §1.16): not re-verified here.
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { secp256k1, schnorr } from '@noble/curves/secp256k1.js';
+import { hash_to_field } from '@noble/curves/abstract/hash-to-curve.js';
+import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha2.js';
+import * as ua from '../src/ua.js';
+import { hex, fromHex, concatBytes, utf8ToBytes, word } from '../src/bytes.js';
+
+const V = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('../../../smartcontracts/vectors/ua/groups.json', import.meta.url)));
+const Pt = secp256k1.Point;
+const N = Pt.Fn.ORDER;
+const G = Pt.BASE;
+const B = (h) => fromHex(h);
+const int = (b) => BigInt(hex(b));
+const eq = (a, b) => a.length === b.length && a.every((x, i) => x === b[i]);
+const attempt = (f) => { try { return f(); } catch { return false; } };
+const u16 = (d, o) => (d[o] << 8) | d[o + 1];
+const mul = (P, k) => P.multiplyUnsafe(((k % N) + N) % N);
+const tagged = (tag, ...parts) => { const t = sha256(utf8ToBytes(tag)); return sha256(concatBytes(t, t, ...parts)); };
+/** SEC1 compressed, prefix 02/03 only. */
+const point = (b) => { if (b.length !== 33 || (b[0] !== 2 && b[0] !== 3)) throw new Error('prefix'); return Pt.fromBytes(b); };
+const enc = (P) => (P.equals(Pt.ZERO) ? new Uint8Array(33) : P.toBytes(true));
+/** RFC 9591 §6.5: hash_to_field, expand_message_xmd SHA-256, DST "FROST-secp256k1-SHA256-v1" ‖ label, 48 bytes, mod N. */
+const H = (label, m) => hash_to_field(m, 1, { DST: utf8ToBytes(`FROST-secp256k1-SHA256-v1${label}`), p: N, m: 1, k: 128, expand: 'xmd', hash: sha256 })[0][0];
+
+// --- musig2-secp256k1 (BIP-327 KeyAgg, §1.6.1) ---
+function keyAgg(pks) {
+  const L = tagged('KeyAgg list', ...pks);
+  const second = pks.find((p) => !eq(p, pks[0]));
+  const a = pks.map((p) => (second && eq(p, second) ? 1n : int(tagged('KeyAgg coefficient', L, p)) % N));
+  const Q = pks.reduce((acc, p, i) => acc.add(mul(point(p), a[i])), Pt.ZERO);
+  return { L, a, Q };
+}
+const members = (kd) => Array.from({ length: u16(kd, 33) }, (_, i) => kd.subarray(35 + 33 * i, 68 + 33 * i));
+/** pop = sig (64) ‖ n × H_i (64): x(P_i) < N, H_i = a_i·P_i (what the ecrecover trick proves), Σ H_i = Q, BIP-340 by x(Q). */
+function musigPop(kd, msg, pop) {
+  const pks = members(kd);
+  if (pop.length !== 64 + 64 * pks.length) return false;
+  const { a } = keyAgg(pks);
+  let S = Pt.ZERO;
+  for (let i = 0; i < pks.length; i++) {
+    if (int(pks[i].subarray(1)) >= N) return false;
+    const h = Pt.fromBytes(concatBytes(Uint8Array.of(4), pop.subarray(64 + 64 * i, 128 + 64 * i)));
+    if (!mul(point(pks[i]), a[i]).equals(h)) return false;
+    S = S.add(h);
+  }
+  return eq(enc(S), kd.subarray(0, 33)) && schnorr.verify(pop.subarray(0, 64), msg, kd.subarray(1, 33));
+}
+
+// --- frost-secp256k1 (RFC 9591, frost-core DKG) ---
+function frostVerify(Y, msg, sig) {
+  if (sig.length !== 65) return false;
+  const R = point(sig.subarray(0, 33));
+  const z = int(sig.subarray(33));
+  if (z >= N) return false;
+  const c = H('chal', concatBytes(sig.subarray(0, 33), Y, msg));
+  return mul(G, z).equals(R.add(mul(point(Y), c)));
+}
+/** frost-core keys::dkg: c_i = HDKG(id_i (32 B) ‖ C_i0 ‖ R_i), R_i == μ_i·G − c_i·C_i0; Y == Σ C_i0. */
+function frostDkg(kd) {
+  const t = u16(kd, 33), n = u16(kd, 35), per = (t + 1) * 33 + 32;
+  let S = Pt.ZERO;
+  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+    const b = kd.subarray(37 + i * per, 37 + (i + 1) * per);
+    const C0 = b.subarray(0, 33), R = b.subarray(t * 33, (t + 1) * 33);
+    const c = H('dkg', concatBytes(word(i + 1), C0, R));
+    if (!point(R).equals(mul(G, int(b.subarray((t + 1) * 33))).subtract(mul(point(C0), c)))) return false;
+    S = S.add(point(C0));
+  }
+  return eq(enc(S), kd.subarray(0, 33));
+}
+
+const SECP = [0x0110, 0x0111];
+const valid = (kind, kd) => attempt(() => (ua.validateKeyData(kind, kd), true));
+const verifyPop = (kind, kd, msg, pop) => valid(kind, kd) && attempt(() =>
+  (kind === 0x0110 ? musigPop(kd, msg, pop) : frostDkg(kd) && frostVerify(kd.subarray(0, 33), msg, pop)));
+const verify = (kind, vk, msg, sig) => attempt(() =>
+  (kind === 0x0110 ? sig.length === 64 && schnorr.verify(sig, msg, point(vk).toBytes(true).subarray(1)) : frostVerify(vk, msg, sig)));
+const A = V.contexts.A;
+const popMsg = (kind, kd) => {
+  const d = ua.popDigest({ chainId: A.chainId, chainUid: B(A.chainUid), registry: B(A.registry), ua: ua.uaOf(kind, kd) });
+  return [d, ua.uaMessage(kind, ua.purposeTag('pop'), d)];
+};
+const secpGroups = V.groups.filter((g) => SECP.includes(g.kind));
+
+test('secp256k1 groups: ids, KeyAgg / DKG recomputed, PoP and signatures verify', () => {
+  assert.equal(secpGroups.length, 10);
+  for (const g of secpGroups) {
+    const kd = B(g.keyData);
+    assert.equal(hex(ua.deriveUA(g.kind, kd)), g.ua, g.name);
+    assert.equal(hex(ua.keyHashOf(g.kind, kd)), g.keyHash, g.name);
+    assert.equal(g.vk, hex(kd.subarray(0, 33)), g.name);
+    const [d, m] = popMsg(g.kind, kd);
+    assert.deepEqual([hex(d), hex(m)], [g.pop.popDigest, g.pop.message], g.name);
+    if (g.kind === 0x0110) {
+      const pks = members(kd);
+      const { L, a, Q } = keyAgg(pks);
+      assert.equal(hex(enc(Q)), g.vk, `${g.name}: Q`);
+      assert.equal(hex(L), g.L);
+      assert.deepEqual(a.map((x) => hex(word(x))), g.coefficients);
+      assert.deepEqual(pks.map((p, i) => hex(mul(point(p), a[i]).toBytes(false).subarray(1))), g.hints);
+      assert.equal(g.pop.blob, hex(concatBytes(B(g.pop.sig), ...g.hints.map(B))));
+    } else {
+      for (const r of g.dkg) {
+        const pre = concatBytes(word(r.id), B(r.commitments[0]), B(r.R));
+        assert.equal(hex(pre), r.preimage);
+        assert.equal(hex(word(H('dkg', pre))), r.challenge, `${g.name} participant ${r.id}`);
+      }
+      assert.equal(g.pop.blob, g.pop.sig);
+    }
+    assert.ok(verifyPop(g.kind, kd, m, B(g.pop.blob)), `${g.name}: verifyPop`);
+    assert.ok(verify(g.kind, kd.subarray(0, 33), m, B(g.pop.sig)), `${g.name}: PoP sig against vk`);
+    for (const s of g.sigs) {
+      const mm = ua.uaMessage(g.kind, ua.purposeTag(s.purpose), B(s.digest));
+      assert.equal(hex(mm), s.message);
+      assert.ok(verify(g.kind, B(g.vk), mm, B(s.sig)), `${g.name} ${s.purpose}`);
+    }
+  }
+});
+
+test('secp256k1 negatives refused for their stated reason', () => {
+  let n = 0;
+  for (const g of secpGroups) for (const x of g.negatives) {
+    const name = `${g.name} ${x.name}`;
+    if (x.entry === 'verify') {
+      assert.equal(verify(g.kind, B(x.vk), B(x.message), B(x.sig)), false, name);
+    } else {
+      const kd = B(x.keyData);
+      const [, m] = popMsg(g.kind, kd);
+      assert.deepEqual([hex(ua.uaOf(g.kind, kd)), hex(m)], [x.ua, x.message], name);
+      const pop = B(x.pop);
+      const sig = pop.subarray(0, g.kind === 0x0110 ? 64 : 65);
+      assert.equal(verify(g.kind, kd.subarray(0, 33), m, sig), x.sigValid, `${name}: sigValid`);
+      if (x.error === 'UAKeyData') assert.equal(valid(g.kind, kd), false, name);
+      else assert.ok(x.error === 'UAPoP' && valid(g.kind, kd) && !verifyPop(g.kind, kd, m, pop), name);
+    }
+    n++;
+  }
+  assert.equal(n, 31);
+});
+
+test('BIP-327 KeyAgg rows (official, incl. duplicate and unsorted lists)', () => {
+  assert.equal(V.bip327.keyAgg.length, 4);
+  for (const r of V.bip327.keyAgg) {
+    const { L, a, Q } = keyAgg(r.keys.map(B));
+    assert.equal(hex(enc(Q)), r.Q);
+    assert.equal(hex(enc(Q).subarray(1)), r.expectedX.toLowerCase());
+    assert.equal(hex(L), r.L);
+    assert.deepEqual(a.map((x) => hex(word(x))), r.coefficients);
+    assert.deepEqual(r.keys.map((k, i) => hex(mul(point(B(k)), a[i]).toBytes(false).subarray(1))), r.hints);
+  }
+  // and the official file's expected keys directly
+  const o = V.bip327.official.key_agg_vectors;
+  for (const c of o.valid_test_cases) assert.equal(hex(enc(keyAgg(c.key_indices.map((i) => B(o.pubkeys[i]))).Q).subarray(1)), `0x${c.expected.toLowerCase()}`);
+});
+
+test('RFC 9591 E.5, the ZF DKG vector, T-FROST', () => {
+  const e = V.rfc9591E5;
+  assert.ok(frostVerify(B(e.groupPublicKey), B(e.message), B(e.sig)));
+  assert.equal(hex(mul(G, int(B(e.groupSecretKey))).toBytes(true)), e.groupPublicKey);
+  assert.ok(valid(0x0111, B(V.zfDkg.keyData)) && frostDkg(B(V.zfDkg.keyData)));
+  assert.equal(V.tFrost.length, 10);
+  for (const r of V.tFrost) {
+    const ok = r.verifier === 'bip340'
+      ? attempt(() => schnorr.verify(B(r.sig), B(r.message), B(r.key)))
+      : attempt(() => frostVerify(B(r.key), B(r.message), B(r.sig)));
+    assert.equal(ok, r.valid, r.name);
+  }
+});

diff --git a/clients/scaas-ua/Cargo.toml b/clients/scaas-ua/Cargo.toml
index 9f8563b..70409d8 100644
--- a/clients/scaas-ua/Cargo.toml
+++ b/clients/scaas-ua/Cargo.toml
@@ -8,7 +8,7 @@ description = "SCaaS Universal Account identity layer: kind table, ids, keyHash,
 
 [dependencies]
 sha3 = "0.10"
-k256 = { version = "0.13", default-features = false, features = ["arithmetic", "ecdsa", "schnorr"] }
+k256 = { version = "0.13", default-features = false, features = ["arithmetic", "ecdsa", "schnorr", "hash2curve"] }
 p256 = { version = "0.13", default-features = false, features = ["arithmetic", "ecdsa"] }
 sha2 = "=0.10.9"
 curve25519-dalek = "4"
@@ -17,10 +17,22 @@ ark-ff = "0.5"
 ark-ec = "0.5"
 ark-bn254 = "0.5"
 ark-bls12-381 = "0.5"
+# feature `signers`: BIP-327 MuSig2 (+ the bn254 port) and RFC 9591 FROST with the DKG (ZF frost-core), for groups.json
+frost-core = { version = "=3.0.0", default-features = false, optional = true }
+frost-secp256k1 = { version = "=3.0.0", default-features = false, optional = true }
+rand_core = { version = "0.6", optional = true }
+rand_chacha = { version = "=0.3.1", optional = true }
+
+[features]
+signers = ["dep:frost-core", "dep:frost-secp256k1", "dep:rand_core", "dep:rand_chacha"]
 
 [dev-dependencies]
 hex = "0.4"
-serde_json = "1"
+serde_json = { version = "1", features = ["preserve_order"] }
+
+[[test]]
+name = "groups"
+required-features = ["signers"]
 
 # arkworks subgroup checks crawl unoptimised
 [profile.dev.package."*"]
diff --git a/clients/scaas-ua/src/frost.rs b/clients/scaas-ua/src/frost.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5ee3b37
--- /dev/null
+++ b/clients/scaas-ua/src/frost.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,203 @@
+//! RFC 9591 FROST with the DKG of ZF `frost-core` (accounts.md §1.6.1): FROST(secp256k1, SHA-256) is ZF
+//! `frost-secp256k1` ([`Secp256K1Sha256`]); FROST(bn254, keccak256) is the [`Bn254Keccak256`] ciphersuite defined here
+//! per the spec table: `contextString = "FROST-BN254-KECCAK256-SCAAS-v1"`, elements 64-byte EIP-196 `x ‖ y`, scalars
+//! 32-byte big-endian, `H1/H3/H4/H5/HDKG(m) = keccak256(cs ‖ label ‖ m ‖ 0x00) ‖ keccak256(cs ‖ label ‖ m ‖ 0x01) mod r`
+//! (labels `rho`, `nonce`, `msg`, `com`, `dkg`; `H4`/`H5` output that scalar's 32 bytes), `H2` = the scaas Schnorr
+//! challenge, so a signature verifies as `schnorr-bn254`. No `HID` (identifiers are the scalars 1..n). Plus
+//! [`dkg`] / [`Dkg::sign`]: every participant simulated in one process, for groups.json. Feature `signers`. UNAUDITED.
+use crate::keccak;
+use crate::musig2::{bn_parse, bn_schnorr_challenge, bn_ser};
+use frost_core::keys::dkg::{part1, part2, part3, round1};
+use frost_core::keys::{KeyPackage, PublicKeyPackage};
+use frost_core::{Ciphersuite, Error, Field, FieldError, Group, GroupError, Identifier, Signature, SigningPackage};
+pub use frost_secp256k1::Secp256K1Sha256;
+use rand_core::{CryptoRng, RngCore};
+use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+
+pub const BN254_CONTEXT: &str = "FROST-BN254-KECCAK256-SCAAS-v1";
+
+#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
+pub struct Bn254ScalarField;
+
+impl Field for Bn254ScalarField {
+    type Scalar = ark_bn254::Fr;
+    type Serialization = [u8; 32];
+    fn zero() -> Self::Scalar {
+        ark_bn254::Fr::from(0u64)
+    }
+    fn one() -> Self::Scalar {
+        ark_bn254::Fr::from(1u64)
+    }
+    fn invert(s: &Self::Scalar) -> Result<Self::Scalar, FieldError> {
+        ark_ff::Field::inverse(s).ok_or(FieldError::InvalidZeroScalar)
+    }
+    fn random<R: RngCore + CryptoRng>(rng: &mut R) -> Self::Scalar {
+        use ark_ff::PrimeField;
+        let mut b = [0u8; 64];
+        rng.fill_bytes(&mut b);
+        ark_bn254::Fr::from_be_bytes_mod_order(&b)
+    }
+    fn serialize(s: &Self::Scalar) -> Self::Serialization {
+        use crate::musig2::{Bn254, Curve};
+        Bn254::s_bytes(s)
+    }
+    fn little_endian_serialize(s: &Self::Scalar) -> Self::Serialization {
+        let mut b = Self::serialize(s);
+        b.reverse();
+        b
+    }
+    fn deserialize(b: &Self::Serialization) -> Result<Self::Scalar, FieldError> {
+        use crate::musig2::{Bn254, Curve};
+        Bn254::s_parse(b).ok_or(FieldError::MalformedScalar)
+    }
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct Bn254Group;
+
+impl Group for Bn254Group {
+    type Field = Bn254ScalarField;
+    type Element = ark_bn254::G1Projective;
+    type Serialization = [u8; 64];
+    fn cofactor() -> ark_bn254::Fr {
+        ark_bn254::Fr::from(1u64)
+    }
+    fn identity() -> Self::Element {
+        ark_ff::Zero::zero()
+    }
+    fn generator() -> Self::Element {
+        ark_ec::PrimeGroup::generator()
+    }
+    fn serialize(e: &Self::Element) -> Result<Self::Serialization, GroupError> {
+        if *e == Self::identity() {
+            return Err(GroupError::InvalidIdentityElement);
+        }
+        Ok(bn_ser(e).try_into().expect("64 bytes"))
+    }
+    fn deserialize(b: &Self::Serialization) -> Result<Self::Element, GroupError> {
+        if b.iter().all(|x| *x == 0) {
+            return Err(GroupError::InvalidIdentityElement);
+        }
+        bn_parse(b).ok_or(GroupError::MalformedElement)
+    }
+}
+
+/// `keccak256(cs ‖ label ‖ m ‖ 0x00) ‖ keccak256(cs ‖ label ‖ m ‖ 0x01)` mod r.
+pub fn bn254_hash(label: &[u8], m: &[u8]) -> ark_bn254::Fr {
+    use ark_ff::PrimeField;
+    let h = |b: u8| keccak(&[BN254_CONTEXT.as_bytes(), label, m, &[b]]);
+    ark_bn254::Fr::from_be_bytes_mod_order(&[h(0), h(1)].concat())
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
+pub struct Bn254Keccak256;
+
+impl Ciphersuite for Bn254Keccak256 {
+    const ID: &'static str = BN254_CONTEXT;
+    type Group = Bn254Group;
+    type HashOutput = [u8; 32];
+    type SignatureSerialization = [u8; 96];
+    fn H1(m: &[u8]) -> ark_bn254::Fr {
+        bn254_hash(b"rho", m)
+    }
+    /// frost-core passes `m = R ‖ Y ‖ msg`: exactly the scaas Schnorr transcript.
+    fn H2(m: &[u8]) -> ark_bn254::Fr {
+        bn_schnorr_challenge(m)
+    }
+    fn H3(m: &[u8]) -> ark_bn254::Fr {
+        bn254_hash(b"nonce", m)
+    }
+    fn H4(m: &[u8]) -> [u8; 32] {
+        Bn254ScalarField::serialize(&bn254_hash(b"msg", m))
+    }
+    fn H5(m: &[u8]) -> [u8; 32] {
+        Bn254ScalarField::serialize(&bn254_hash(b"com", m))
+    }
+    fn HDKG(m: &[u8]) -> Option<ark_bn254::Fr> {
+        Some(bn254_hash(b"dkg", m))
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn id<C: Ciphersuite>(i: u16) -> Identifier<C> {
+    Identifier::try_from(i).expect("nonzero")
+}
+
+/// One DKG run (frost-core `part1/2/3`) with participants `1..=n`.
+pub struct Dkg<C: Ciphersuite> {
+    pub t: u16,
+    pub n: u16,
+    pub round1: BTreeMap<Identifier<C>, round1::Package<C>>,
+    pub keys: BTreeMap<Identifier<C>, KeyPackage<C>>,
+    pub public: PublicKeyPackage<C>,
+}
+
+fn others<K: Ord + Copy, V: Clone>(m: &BTreeMap<K, V>, me: &K) -> BTreeMap<K, V> {
+    m.iter().filter(|(k, _)| *k != me).map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.clone())).collect()
+}
+
+pub fn dkg<C: Ciphersuite, R: RngCore + CryptoRng>(t: u16, n: u16, rng: &mut R) -> Result<Dkg<C>, Error<C>> {
+    let mut sec1 = BTreeMap::new();
+    let mut round1 = BTreeMap::new();
+    for i in 1..=n {
+        let (s, p) = part1::<C, _>(id(i), n, t, &mut *rng)?;
+        sec1.insert(id::<C>(i), s);
+        round1.insert(id::<C>(i), p);
+    }
+    let mut sec2 = BTreeMap::new();
+    let mut inbox: BTreeMap<Identifier<C>, BTreeMap<Identifier<C>, _>> = BTreeMap::new();
+    for (me, s) in sec1 {
+        let (s2, out) = part2(s, &others(&round1, &me))?;
+        sec2.insert(me, s2);
+        for (to, pkg) in out {
+            inbox.entry(to).or_default().insert(me, pkg);
+        }
+    }
+    let mut keys = BTreeMap::new();
+    let mut public = None;
+    for (me, s2) in &sec2 {
+        let (kp, pkp) = part3(s2, &others(&round1, me), &inbox[me])?;
+        assert!(public.as_ref().is_none_or(|p| *p == pkp), "participants disagree on the public key package");
+        public = Some(pkp);
+        keys.insert(*me, kp);
+    }
+    Ok(Dkg { t, n, round1, keys, public: public.expect("n ≥ 2") })
+}
+
+impl<C: Ciphersuite> Dkg<C> {
+    /// Registry key data `Y ‖ u16 t ‖ u16 n ‖ n × (t × C_ik ‖ R_i ‖ μ_i)`.
+    pub fn key_data(&self) -> Vec<u8> {
+        let mut d = self.public.verifying_key().serialize().expect("Y");
+        d.extend(self.t.to_be_bytes());
+        d.extend(self.n.to_be_bytes());
+        for p in self.round1.values() {
+            d.extend(p.commitment().serialize_whole().expect("commitments"));
+            d.extend(p.proof_of_knowledge().serialize().expect("PoK"));
+        }
+        d
+    }
+
+    /// The group secret (Σ a_i0), reconstructed from `t` shares.
+    pub fn group_secret(&self) -> frost_core::SigningKey<C> {
+        let kps: Vec<KeyPackage<C>> = self.keys.values().take(self.t as usize).cloned().collect();
+        frost_core::keys::reconstruct(&kps).expect("t shares")
+    }
+
+    /// RFC 9591 two-round signing by `signers`, aggregated and checked against `Y`.
+    pub fn sign<R: RngCore + CryptoRng>(&self, signers: &[u16], msg: &[u8], rng: &mut R) -> Result<Signature<C>, Error<C>> {
+        let mut nonces = BTreeMap::new();
+        let mut commitments = BTreeMap::new();
+        for i in signers {
+            let (n, c) = frost_core::round1::commit(self.keys[&id(*i)].signing_share(), &mut *rng);
+            nonces.insert(id::<C>(*i), n);
+            commitments.insert(id::<C>(*i), c);
+        }
+        let package = SigningPackage::new(commitments, msg);
+        let mut shares = BTreeMap::new();
+        for (i, n) in &nonces {
+            shares.insert(*i, frost_core::round2::sign(&package, n, &self.keys[i])?);
+        }
+        let sig = frost_core::aggregate(&package, &shares, &self.public)?;
+        self.public.verifying_key().verify(msg, &sig)?;
+        Ok(sig)
+    }
+}
diff --git a/clients/scaas-ua/src/groups.rs b/clients/scaas-ua/src/groups.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5a7fb04
--- /dev/null
+++ b/clients/scaas-ua/src/groups.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,204 @@
+//! The registry's checks for the group kinds 0x0110–0x0113 (accounts.md §1.6.1, §1.9), written from the spec as the EVM
+//! verifiers apply them, independent of the signers (`musig2.rs`, frost-core): `verify(vk, msg, sig)` against the stored
+//! group key, `verifyPop(keyData, msg, pop)` = the whole group check + the group signature. MuSig2-secp256k1 hints are
+//! checked the way the `ecrecover` trick does (`x(P_i) < N`, recovered point `(a_i·x · x⁻¹)·lift(x, parity)` compared by
+//! address). Untrusted input: anything malformed is `false`, never a panic. UNAUDITED.
+use crate::verify::{bip340, schnorr, sha256};
+use crate::{keccak, validate_key_data};
+use ark_ec::{AffineRepr, CurveGroup};
+use ark_ff::PrimeField;
+use k256::elliptic_curve::hash2curve::{hash_to_field, ExpandMsgXmd};
+use k256::elliptic_curve::ops::Reduce;
+use k256::elliptic_curve::sec1::{FromEncodedPoint, ToEncodedPoint};
+use k256::elliptic_curve::PrimeField as _;
+use k256::{AffinePoint, ProjectivePoint, Scalar};
+
+pub const FROST_SECP256K1_CONTEXT: &[u8] = b"FROST-secp256k1-SHA256-v1";
+pub const FROST_BN254_CONTEXT: &[u8] = b"FROST-BN254-KECCAK256-SCAAS-v1";
+
+type Bn = ark_bn254::g1::Config;
+
+/// `verify` (`pop = false`: `key` = the stored `vk`) / `verifyPop` (`pop = true`: `key` = the key data).
+pub(crate) fn verify(kind: u16, key: &[u8], msg: &[u8; 32], sig: &[u8], pop: bool) -> bool {
+    if pop {
+        return validate_key_data(kind, key, false).is_ok()
+            && match kind {
+                0x0110 => musig2_secp256k1_pop(key, msg, sig),
+                0x0111 => dkg_check(key, 33, secp_point, compressed, secp_pok) && frost_secp256k1(&key[..33], msg, sig),
+                0x0112 => musig2_bn254_pop(key, msg, sig),
+                _ => dkg_check(key, 64, bn_point, bn_bytes, bn_pok) && schnorr::<Bn>(&key[..64], msg, sig, 32),
+            };
+    }
+    match kind {
+        0x0110 => key.len() == 33 && (key[0] == 2 || key[0] == 3) && bip340(&key[1..], msg, sig),
+        0x0111 => frost_secp256k1(key, msg, sig),
+        _ => schnorr::<Bn>(key, msg, sig, 32),
+    }
+}
+
+/// The FROST registration check without the signature: every PoK and `Y == Σ C_i0` (key data validated first).
+pub fn dkg_transcript_ok(kind: u16, kd: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    validate_key_data(kind, kd, false).is_ok()
+        && match kind {
+            0x0111 => dkg_check(kd, 33, secp_point, compressed, secp_pok),
+            0x0113 => dkg_check(kd, 64, bn_point, bn_bytes, bn_pok),
+            _ => false,
+        }
+}
+
+fn tagged(tag: &str, parts: &[&[u8]]) -> [u8; 32] {
+    let t = sha256(&[tag.as_bytes()]);
+    sha256(&[&[&t[..], &t[..]], parts].concat())
+}
+
+fn u16_at(d: &[u8], o: usize) -> usize {
+    ((d[o] as usize) << 8) | d[o + 1] as usize
+}
+
+// --- secp256k1 ---
+
+fn secp_point(b: &[u8]) -> Option<ProjectivePoint> {
+    if b.len() != 33 || !(b[0] == 2 || b[0] == 3) {
+        return None;
+    }
+    let ep = k256::EncodedPoint::from_bytes(b).ok()?;
+    Option::<AffinePoint>::from(AffinePoint::from_encoded_point(&ep)).map(Into::into)
+}
+fn secp_scalar(b: &[u8]) -> Option<Scalar> {
+    Scalar::from_repr(<[u8; 32]>::try_from(b).ok()?.into()).into()
+}
+/// SEC1 compressed; the identity as 33 zero bytes (never equal to valid key data).
+fn compressed(p: &ProjectivePoint) -> Vec<u8> {
+    if *p == ProjectivePoint::IDENTITY {
+        return vec![0; 33];
+    }
+    p.to_affine().to_encoded_point(true).as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+fn address(xy: &[u8]) -> [u8; 20] {
+    keccak(&[xy])[12..].try_into().expect("20")
+}
+
+/// RFC 9591 `hash_to_field` (`expand_message_xmd` SHA-256, 48 bytes, mod N) with DST `contextString ‖ label`.
+fn secp_h(label: &[u8], m: &[u8]) -> Scalar {
+    let mut u = [Scalar::ZERO];
+    hash_to_field::<ExpandMsgXmd<sha2::Sha256>, Scalar>(&[m], &[FROST_SECP256K1_CONTEXT, label], &mut u).expect("xmd");
+    u[0]
+}
+
+/// RFC 9591 FROST(secp256k1, SHA-256) verify: `Y` and `R` SEC1 compressed, `z < N`, `z·G == R + H2(R ‖ Y ‖ msg)·Y`.
+pub fn frost_secp256k1(y: &[u8], msg: &[u8], sig: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    if sig.len() != 65 {
+        return false;
+    }
+    let (Some(yp), Some(r), Some(z)) = (secp_point(y), secp_point(&sig[..33]), secp_scalar(&sig[33..])) else { return false };
+    let c = secp_h(b"chal", &[&sig[..33], y, msg].concat());
+    ProjectivePoint::GENERATOR * z == r + yp * c
+}
+
+/// `pop = sig (64) ‖ n × H_i (64, x ‖ y)`: KeyAgg recomputed, each `H_i = a_i·P_i` checked as `ecrecover` would,
+/// `Σ H_i == Q` (x and parity), then BIP-340 by `x(Q)`.
+fn musig2_secp256k1_pop(kd: &[u8], msg: &[u8; 32], pop: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    let n = u16_at(kd, 33);
+    if pop.len() != 64 + 64 * n {
+        return false;
+    }
+    let pks: Vec<&[u8]> = kd[35..].chunks(33).collect();
+    let l = tagged("KeyAgg list", &pks);
+    let second = pks.iter().find(|p| **p != pks[0]);
+    let mut sum = ProjectivePoint::IDENTITY;
+    for (i, pk) in pks.iter().enumerate() {
+        let a = if Some(pk) == second {
+            Scalar::ONE
+        } else {
+            <Scalar as Reduce<k256::U256>>::reduce_bytes(&tagged("KeyAgg coefficient", &[&l, pk]).into())
+        };
+        // ecrecover(0, v, r = x(P_i), s = a·r mod N) = r⁻¹·s·R: needs r < N; R = lift(r, v) = P_i
+        let (Some(r), Some(p)) = (secp_scalar(&pk[1..]), secp_point(pk)) else { return false };
+        let Some(r_inv) = Option::<Scalar>::from(r.invert()) else { return false }; // ecrecover refuses r = 0
+        let rec = p * (a * r * r_inv);
+        let h = &pop[64 + 64 * i..128 + 64 * i];
+        if address(&rec.to_affine().to_encoded_point(false).as_bytes()[1..]) != address(h) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        sum += rec; // = H_i (address match); the EVM adds H_i in affine coordinates
+    }
+    compressed(&sum) == kd[..33] && bip340(&kd[1..33], msg, &pop[..64])
+}
+
+fn secp_pok(id: u16, c0: &[u8], r: &[u8], mu: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    let (Some(c0p), Some(rp), Some(mu)) = (secp_point(c0), secp_point(r), secp_scalar(mu)) else { return false };
+    let c = secp_h(b"dkg", &[&Scalar::from(id as u64).to_bytes()[..], c0, r].concat());
+    rp == ProjectivePoint::GENERATOR * mu - c0p * c
+}
+
+// --- bn254 ---
+
+fn bn_point(b: &[u8]) -> Option<ark_bn254::G1Projective> {
+    crate::verify::g1::<Bn>(b, 32).map(Into::into)
+}
+fn bn_bytes(p: &ark_bn254::G1Projective) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use ark_ff::BigInteger;
+    let a = p.into_affine();
+    match a.xy() {
+        Some((x, y)) => [x.into_bigint().to_bytes_be(), y.into_bigint().to_bytes_be()].concat(),
+        None => vec![0; 64],
+    }
+}
+fn fr(b: &[u8]) -> Option<ark_bn254::Fr> {
+    use ark_ff::BigInteger;
+    let f = ark_bn254::Fr::from_be_bytes_mod_order(b);
+    (b.len() == 32 && f.into_bigint().to_bytes_be() == b).then_some(f)
+}
+fn fr_wide(h: impl Fn(u8) -> [u8; 32]) -> ark_bn254::Fr {
+    ark_bn254::Fr::from_be_bytes_mod_order(&[h(0), h(1)].concat())
+}
+
+/// KeyAgg of the bn254 port (no hints: `ECMUL`/`ECADD`), `Q` equal, then scaas Schnorr by `Q`.
+fn musig2_bn254_pop(kd: &[u8], msg: &[u8; 32], pop: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    let pks: Vec<&[u8]> = kd[66..].chunks(64).collect();
+    let l = tagged("KeyAgg list", &pks);
+    let second = pks.iter().find(|p| **p != pks[0]);
+    let mut q = ark_bn254::G1Projective::default();
+    for pk in &pks {
+        let a = if Some(pk) == second {
+            ark_bn254::Fr::from(1u64)
+        } else {
+            fr_wide(|b| tagged("KeyAgg coefficient", &[&l, pk, &[b]]))
+        };
+        let Some(p) = bn_point(pk) else { return false };
+        q += p * a;
+    }
+    bn_bytes(&q) == kd[..64] && schnorr::<Bn>(&kd[..64], msg, pop, 32)
+}
+
+fn bn_pok(id: u16, c0: &[u8], r: &[u8], mu: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    let (Some(c0p), Some(rp), Some(mu)) = (bn_point(c0), bn_point(r), fr(mu)) else { return false };
+    let mut idb = [0u8; 32];
+    idb[30..].copy_from_slice(&id.to_be_bytes());
+    let c = fr_wide(|b| keccak(&[FROST_BN254_CONTEXT, b"dkg", &idb, c0, r, &[b]]));
+    rp == ark_bn254::G1Affine::generator() * mu - c0p * c
+}
+
+// --- FROST DKG transcript ---
+
+/// Every PoK `R_i == μ_i·G − c_i·C_i0`, `c_i = H_dkg(id_i ‖ C_i0 ‖ R_i)`, and `Y == Σ C_i0` (compared as encodings).
+fn dkg_check<P: core::ops::Add<Output = P> + Copy>(
+    kd: &[u8],
+    w: usize,
+    point: fn(&[u8]) -> Option<P>,
+    enc: fn(&P) -> Vec<u8>,
+    pok: fn(u16, &[u8], &[u8], &[u8]) -> bool,
+) -> bool {
+    let (t, n) = (u16_at(kd, w), u16_at(kd, w + 2));
+    let per = (t + 1) * w + 32;
+    let mut sum: Option<P> = None;
+    for i in 0..n {
+        let b = &kd[w + 4 + i * per..w + 4 + (i + 1) * per];
+        if !pok(i as u16 + 1, &b[..w], &b[t * w..(t + 1) * w], &b[(t + 1) * w..]) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let Some(c0) = point(&b[..w]) else { return false };
+        sum = Some(sum.map_or(c0, |s| s + c0));
+    }
+    sum.is_some_and(|s| enc(&s) == kd[..w])
+}
diff --git a/clients/scaas-ua/src/lib.rs b/clients/scaas-ua/src/lib.rs
index d3794df..ca7bfc4 100644
--- a/clients/scaas-ua/src/lib.rs
+++ b/clients/scaas-ua/src/lib.rs
@@ -2,12 +2,18 @@
 //! `popDigest`, the registry's `UAAuth` digest, member digests, canonical ids and key-data validation. The same rules live
 //! in Solidity (`UAKinds.sol`), JS (clientkeys `ua.js`), Python, Go and C#; `smartcontracts/vectors/ua/` pins all six.
 //! The curve code here (k256, p256, curve25519-dalek, arkworks) is independent of the JS generator's (noble): the vectors
-//! are accepted only when both agree. UNAUDITED.
+//! are accepted only when both agree. Feature `signers` (UA-2c): the MuSig2 / FROST group signers that generate
+//! `groups.json` (`musig2`, `frost`); the group kinds' registry checks are in the default build (`verify`). UNAUDITED.
 use sha3::{Digest, Keccak256};
 
 mod keydata;
 pub use keydata::validate_key_data;
 pub mod verify;
+mod groups;
+#[cfg(feature = "signers")]
+pub mod musig2;
+#[cfg(feature = "signers")]
+pub mod frost;
 
 /// An error whose [`UAError::code`] every implementation and the vector files share.
 #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
diff --git a/clients/scaas-ua/src/musig2.rs b/clients/scaas-ua/src/musig2.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a0bae5a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/clients/scaas-ua/src/musig2.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,385 @@
+//! BIP-327 MuSig2 (accounts.md §1.6.1), one algorithm over two curves: [`Secp256k1`] is BIP-327 itself (checked against
+//! the official vectors, `tests/groups.rs`); [`Bn254`] is the scaas port: points 64-byte EIP-196 `x ‖ y` (aggregate nonce
+//! halves at infinity = 64 zero bytes), scalar hashes widened to 512 bits (`tagged(x ‖ 0x00) ‖ tagged(x ‖ 0x01)` mod r),
+//! no x-only key or parity negation, challenge = the scaas Schnorr challenge, signature `R (64) ‖ s (32)` (verifies as
+//! `schnorr-bn254`). `L = hash_{KeyAgg list}(pk_1 ‖ … ‖ pk_n)` is the plain 32-byte tagged hash on both curves (it is a
+//! byte string, not a scalar). No tweaks (the registry has none). Feature `signers`; test-vector signer, not hardened
+//! (secret nonces are plain bytes). UNAUDITED.
+use crate::keccak;
+use core::ops::{Add, Mul, Neg};
+use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
+
+/// BIP-340 tagged hash `sha256(sha256(tag) ‖ sha256(tag) ‖ parts…)`.
+pub fn tagged(tag: &str, parts: &[&[u8]]) -> [u8; 32] {
+    let t = Sha256::digest(tag.as_bytes());
+    let mut h = Sha256::new();
+    h.update(t);
+    h.update(t);
+    for p in parts {
+        h.update(p);
+    }
+    h.finalize().into()
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 error kinds, named as in the official vectors.
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum Error {
+    /// `invalid_contribution`: `signer` (None for the aggregate nonce) sent a bad `contrib`.
+    Contribution(Option<usize>, &'static str),
+    /// `value`: an out-of-range input.
+    Value(&'static str),
+}
+
+pub trait Curve {
+    type S: Copy + PartialEq + Add<Output = Self::S> + Mul<Output = Self::S> + Neg<Output = Self::S>;
+    type P: Copy + PartialEq + Add<Output = Self::P> + Mul<Self::S, Output = Self::P> + Neg<Output = Self::P>;
+    /// Serialized point length.
+    const PLEN: usize;
+    fn g() -> Self::P;
+    fn inf() -> Self::P;
+    fn one() -> Self::S;
+    fn zero_s() -> Self::S;
+    /// A tagged hash reduced to a scalar.
+    fn hash_s(tag: &str, parts: &[&[u8]]) -> Self::S;
+    fn s_bytes(s: &Self::S) -> [u8; 32];
+    /// Canonical (< order) 32 bytes.
+    fn s_parse(b: &[u8]) -> Option<Self::S>;
+    /// The point, the identity as `PLEN` zero bytes (BIP-327 `cbytes_ext`).
+    fn ser(p: &Self::P) -> Vec<u8>;
+    /// A valid non-identity point.
+    fn parse(b: &[u8]) -> Option<Self::P>;
+    /// BIP-327 `has_even_y`; always true on bn254 (no parity rules).
+    fn even(p: &Self::P) -> bool;
+    /// The aggregate key as hashed into the nonce coefficient: `xbytes(Q)` (secp256k1) or `Q` (bn254).
+    fn agg_bytes(q: &Self::P) -> Vec<u8>;
+    fn challenge(r: &Self::P, q: &Self::P, msg: &[u8]) -> Self::S;
+    fn sig(r: &Self::P, s: &Self::S) -> Vec<u8>;
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 over secp256k1.
+pub struct Secp256k1;
+/// The scaas bn254 port.
+pub struct Bn254;
+
+impl Curve for Secp256k1 {
+    type S = k256::Scalar;
+    type P = k256::ProjectivePoint;
+    const PLEN: usize = 33;
+    fn g() -> Self::P {
+        k256::ProjectivePoint::GENERATOR
+    }
+    fn inf() -> Self::P {
+        k256::ProjectivePoint::IDENTITY
+    }
+    fn one() -> Self::S {
+        k256::Scalar::ONE
+    }
+    fn zero_s() -> Self::S {
+        k256::Scalar::ZERO
+    }
+    fn hash_s(tag: &str, parts: &[&[u8]]) -> Self::S {
+        use k256::elliptic_curve::ops::Reduce;
+        <k256::Scalar as Reduce<k256::U256>>::reduce_bytes(&tagged(tag, parts).into())
+    }
+    fn s_bytes(s: &Self::S) -> [u8; 32] {
+        s.to_bytes().into()
+    }
+    fn s_parse(b: &[u8]) -> Option<Self::S> {
+        use k256::elliptic_curve::PrimeField;
+        let b: [u8; 32] = b.try_into().ok()?;
+        k256::Scalar::from_repr(b.into()).into()
+    }
+    fn ser(p: &Self::P) -> Vec<u8> {
+        use k256::elliptic_curve::sec1::ToEncodedPoint;
+        if *p == Self::inf() {
+            return vec![0; 33];
+        }
+        p.to_affine().to_encoded_point(true).as_bytes().to_vec()
+    }
+    fn parse(b: &[u8]) -> Option<Self::P> {
+        use k256::elliptic_curve::sec1::FromEncodedPoint;
+        if b.len() != 33 || !(b[0] == 2 || b[0] == 3) {
+            return None;
+        }
+        let ep = k256::EncodedPoint::from_bytes(b).ok()?;
+        Option::<k256::AffinePoint>::from(k256::AffinePoint::from_encoded_point(&ep)).map(Into::into)
+    }
+    fn even(p: &Self::P) -> bool {
+        use k256::elliptic_curve::point::AffineCoordinates;
+        !bool::from(p.to_affine().y_is_odd())
+    }
+    fn agg_bytes(q: &Self::P) -> Vec<u8> {
+        Self::ser(q)[1..].to_vec()
+    }
+    fn challenge(r: &Self::P, q: &Self::P, msg: &[u8]) -> Self::S {
+        Self::hash_s("BIP0340/challenge", &[&Self::ser(r)[1..], &Self::ser(q)[1..], msg])
+    }
+    fn sig(r: &Self::P, s: &Self::S) -> Vec<u8> {
+        [&Self::ser(r)[1..], &Self::s_bytes(s)[..]].concat()
+    }
+}
+
+/// `x ‖ y`, 32-byte big-endian each; the identity as 64 zero bytes.
+pub fn bn_ser(p: &ark_bn254::G1Projective) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use ark_ec::CurveGroup;
+    use ark_ff::{BigInteger, PrimeField};
+    let a = p.into_affine();
+    if a.infinity {
+        return vec![0; 64];
+    }
+    [a.x.into_bigint().to_bytes_be(), a.y.into_bigint().to_bytes_be()].concat()
+}
+
+/// Canonical coordinates, on the curve, not the identity (bn254 G1 has cofactor 1).
+pub fn bn_parse(b: &[u8]) -> Option<ark_bn254::G1Projective> {
+    use ark_ff::{BigInteger, PrimeField, Zero};
+    if b.len() != 64 {
+        return None;
+    }
+    let fe = |s: &[u8]| {
+        let f = ark_bn254::Fq::from_be_bytes_mod_order(s);
+        (f.into_bigint().to_bytes_be() == s).then_some(f)
+    };
+    let (x, y) = (fe(&b[..32])?, fe(&b[32..])?);
+    let p = ark_bn254::G1Affine::new_unchecked(x, y);
+    (!(x.is_zero() && y.is_zero()) && p.is_on_curve()).then(|| p.into())
+}
+
+/// The scaas Schnorr challenge `(keccak(s ‖ 2) · 2^256 + keccak(s ‖ 3)) mod r`, `s = keccak(tag ‖ m)`.
+pub fn bn_schnorr_challenge(m: &[u8]) -> ark_bn254::Fr {
+    use ark_ff::PrimeField;
+    let seed = keccak(&[crate::verify::SCHNORR_TAG, m]);
+    ark_bn254::Fr::from_be_bytes_mod_order(&[keccak(&[&seed, &[2]]), keccak(&[&seed, &[3]])].concat())
+}
+
+impl Curve for Bn254 {
+    type S = ark_bn254::Fr;
+    type P = ark_bn254::G1Projective;
+    const PLEN: usize = 64;
+    fn g() -> Self::P {
+        use ark_ec::PrimeGroup;
+        ark_bn254::G1Projective::generator()
+    }
+    fn inf() -> Self::P {
+        use ark_ff::Zero;
+        ark_bn254::G1Projective::zero()
+    }
+    fn one() -> Self::S {
+        ark_bn254::Fr::from(1u64)
+    }
+    fn zero_s() -> Self::S {
+        ark_bn254::Fr::from(0u64)
+    }
+    fn hash_s(tag: &str, parts: &[&[u8]]) -> Self::S {
+        use ark_ff::PrimeField;
+        let wide = |b: u8| tagged(tag, &[parts, &[&[b][..]]].concat());
+        ark_bn254::Fr::from_be_bytes_mod_order(&[wide(0), wide(1)].concat())
+    }
+    fn s_bytes(s: &Self::S) -> [u8; 32] {
+        use ark_ff::{BigInteger, PrimeField};
+        s.into_bigint().to_bytes_be().try_into().expect("32 bytes")
+    }
+    fn s_parse(b: &[u8]) -> Option<Self::S> {
+        use ark_ff::{BigInteger, PrimeField};
+        let f = ark_bn254::Fr::from_be_bytes_mod_order(b);
+        (b.len() == 32 && f.into_bigint().to_bytes_be() == b).then_some(f)
+    }
+    fn ser(p: &Self::P) -> Vec<u8> {
+        bn_ser(p)
+    }
+    fn parse(b: &[u8]) -> Option<Self::P> {
+        bn_parse(b)
+    }
+    fn even(_: &Self::P) -> bool {
+        true
+    }
+    fn agg_bytes(q: &Self::P) -> Vec<u8> {
+        bn_ser(q)
+    }
+    fn challenge(r: &Self::P, q: &Self::P, msg: &[u8]) -> Self::S {
+        bn_schnorr_challenge(&[&bn_ser(r)[..], &bn_ser(q), msg].concat())
+    }
+    fn sig(r: &Self::P, s: &Self::S) -> Vec<u8> {
+        [bn_ser(r), Self::s_bytes(s).to_vec()].concat()
+    }
+}
+
+/// KeyAgg output: `Q`, `L` and the second distinct key (BIP-327 `get_second_key`, all zero if none).
+pub struct KeyAgg<C: Curve> {
+    pub q: C::P,
+    pub l: [u8; 32],
+    pub second: Vec<u8>,
+}
+
+impl<C: Curve> KeyAgg<C> {
+    /// `a_i`: 1 for the second distinct key, else `hash_{KeyAgg coefficient}(L ‖ pk) mod order`.
+    pub fn coef(&self, pk: &[u8]) -> C::S {
+        if pk == self.second.as_slice() { C::one() } else { C::hash_s("KeyAgg coefficient", &[&self.l, pk]) }
+    }
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 KeySort: lexicographic byte order.
+pub fn key_sort(pks: &mut [Vec<u8>]) {
+    pks.sort();
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 KeyAgg (no tweaks).
+pub fn key_agg<C: Curve>(pks: &[Vec<u8>]) -> Result<KeyAgg<C>, Error> {
+    let parts: Vec<&[u8]> = pks.iter().map(|p| p.as_slice()).collect();
+    let l = tagged("KeyAgg list", &parts);
+    let second = pks.iter().find(|p| **p != pks[0]).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| vec![0; C::PLEN]);
+    let mut ka = KeyAgg::<C> { q: C::inf(), l, second };
+    for (i, pk) in pks.iter().enumerate() {
+        let p = C::parse(pk).ok_or(Error::Contribution(Some(i), "pubkey"))?;
+        ka.q = ka.q + p * ka.coef(pk);
+    }
+    if ka.q == C::inf() {
+        return Err(Error::Value("The result of KeyAgg cannot be infinity."));
+    }
+    Ok(ka)
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 NonceGen: `(secnonce = k1 ‖ k2 ‖ pk, pubnonce = R1 ‖ R2)`. `aggpk` is `xbytes(Q)` / `Q`.
+pub fn nonce_gen<C: Curve>(
+    rand_: &[u8; 32],
+    sk: Option<&[u8]>,
+    pk: &[u8],
+    aggpk: Option<&[u8]>,
+    msg: Option<&[u8]>,
+    extra_in: Option<&[u8]>,
+) -> (Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>) {
+    let rand: Vec<u8> = match sk {
+        Some(sk) => sk.iter().zip(tagged("MuSig/aux", &[rand_])).map(|(a, b)| a ^ b).collect(),
+        None => rand_.to_vec(),
+    };
+    let aggpk = aggpk.unwrap_or(&[]);
+    let msg_prefixed = match msg {
+        None => vec![0u8],
+        Some(m) => [&[1u8][..], &(m.len() as u64).to_be_bytes(), m].concat(),
+    };
+    let extra = extra_in.unwrap_or(&[]);
+    let k = |i: u8| {
+        C::hash_s(
+            "MuSig/nonce",
+            &[&rand, &[pk.len() as u8], pk, &[aggpk.len() as u8], aggpk, &msg_prefixed, &(extra.len() as u32).to_be_bytes(), extra, &[i]],
+        )
+    };
+    let (k1, k2) = (k(0), k(1));
+    let secnonce = [&C::s_bytes(&k1)[..], &C::s_bytes(&k2), pk].concat();
+    (secnonce, [C::ser(&(C::g() * k1)), C::ser(&(C::g() * k2))].concat())
+}
+
+/// A point or, for `PLEN` zero bytes, the identity (BIP-327 `cpoint_ext`).
+fn parse_ext<C: Curve>(b: &[u8]) -> Option<C::P> {
+    if b.iter().all(|x| *x == 0) { Some(C::inf()) } else { C::parse(b) }
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 NonceAgg.
+pub fn nonce_agg<C: Curve>(pubnonces: &[Vec<u8>]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error> {
+    let mut out = Vec::new();
+    for j in 0..2 {
+        let mut r = C::inf();
+        for (i, pn) in pubnonces.iter().enumerate() {
+            let p = pn.get(j * C::PLEN..(j + 1) * C::PLEN).and_then(C::parse);
+            r = r + p.ok_or(Error::Contribution(Some(i), "pubnonce"))?;
+        }
+        out.extend(C::ser(&r));
+    }
+    Ok(out)
+}
+
+/// KeyAgg, `b`, `R`, `e`.
+pub type Session<C> = (KeyAgg<C>, <C as Curve>::S, <C as Curve>::P, <C as Curve>::S);
+
+/// BIP-327 GetSessionValues (no tweaks).
+pub fn session<C: Curve>(aggnonce: &[u8], pks: &[Vec<u8>], msg: &[u8]) -> Result<Session<C>, Error> {
+    let ka = key_agg::<C>(pks)?;
+    let b = C::hash_s("MuSig/noncecoef", &[aggnonce, &C::agg_bytes(&ka.q), msg]);
+    let bad = Error::Contribution(None, "aggnonce");
+    if aggnonce.len() != 2 * C::PLEN {
+        return Err(bad);
+    }
+    let r1 = parse_ext::<C>(&aggnonce[..C::PLEN]).ok_or(bad.clone())?;
+    let r2 = parse_ext::<C>(&aggnonce[C::PLEN..]).ok_or(bad)?;
+    let r_ = r1 + r2 * b;
+    let r = if r_ == C::inf() { C::g() } else { r_ };
+    let e = C::challenge(&r, &ka.q, msg);
+    Ok((ka, b, r, e))
+}
+
+fn sign_g<C: Curve>(q: &C::P) -> C::S {
+    if C::even(q) { C::one() } else { -C::one() }
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 Sign: the partial signature `s` (32 bytes).
+pub fn sign<C: Curve>(secnonce: &[u8], sk: &[u8], aggnonce: &[u8], pks: &[Vec<u8>], msg: &[u8]) -> Result<[u8; 32], Error> {
+    let (ka, b, r, e) = session::<C>(aggnonce, pks, msg)?;
+    let nonzero = |s: Option<C::S>| s.filter(|s| *s != C::zero_s());
+    let k1 = nonzero(C::s_parse(&secnonce[..32])).ok_or(Error::Value("first secnonce value is out of range."))?;
+    let k2 = nonzero(C::s_parse(&secnonce[32..64])).ok_or(Error::Value("second secnonce value is out of range."))?;
+    let (k1, k2) = if C::even(&r) { (k1, k2) } else { (-k1, -k2) };
+    let d_ = nonzero(C::s_parse(sk)).ok_or(Error::Value("The secret key must be in the range 0 < sk < n."))?;
+    let pk = C::ser(&(C::g() * d_));
+    if pk != secnonce[64..] {
+        return Err(Error::Value("Public key does not match nonce_gen argument"));
+    }
+    if !pks.contains(&pk) {
+        return Err(Error::Value("The signer's pubkey must be included in the list of pubkeys."));
+    }
+    let d = sign_g::<C>(&ka.q) * d_;
+    Ok(C::s_bytes(&(k1 + b * k2 + e * ka.coef(&pk) * d)))
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 PartialSigVerifyInternal.
+pub fn partial_sig_verify_internal<C: Curve>(
+    psig: &[u8],
+    pubnonce: &[u8],
+    pk: &[u8],
+    aggnonce: &[u8],
+    pks: &[Vec<u8>],
+    msg: &[u8],
+) -> Result<bool, Error> {
+    let (ka, b, r, e) = session::<C>(aggnonce, pks, msg)?;
+    let Some(s) = C::s_parse(psig) else { return Ok(false) };
+    let (Some(r1), Some(r2), Some(p)) = (C::parse(&pubnonce[..C::PLEN]), C::parse(&pubnonce[C::PLEN..]), C::parse(pk)) else {
+        return Ok(false);
+    };
+    let re = r1 + r2 * b;
+    let re = if C::even(&r) { re } else { -re };
+    Ok(C::g() * s == re + p * (e * ka.coef(pk) * sign_g::<C>(&ka.q)))
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 PartialSigVerify: signer `i`'s `psig` against all public nonces.
+pub fn partial_sig_verify<C: Curve>(psig: &[u8], pubnonces: &[Vec<u8>], pks: &[Vec<u8>], msg: &[u8], i: usize) -> Result<bool, Error> {
+    let aggnonce = nonce_agg::<C>(pubnonces)?;
+    partial_sig_verify_internal::<C>(psig, &pubnonces[i], &pks[i], &aggnonce, pks, msg)
+}
+
+/// BIP-327 PartialSigAgg (no tweaks): the final signature.
+pub fn partial_sig_agg<C: Curve>(psigs: &[[u8; 32]], aggnonce: &[u8], pks: &[Vec<u8>], msg: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error> {
+    let (_, _, r, _) = session::<C>(aggnonce, pks, msg)?;
+    let mut s = C::zero_s();
+    for (i, p) in psigs.iter().enumerate() {
+        s = s + C::s_parse(p).ok_or(Error::Contribution(Some(i), "psig"))?;
+    }
+    Ok(C::sig(&r, &s))
+}
+
+/// The whole n-of-n protocol for one message, every signer simulated: `rands[i]` is signer i's NonceGen `rand'`.
+/// Returns the signature; every partial signature is verified on the way.
+pub fn sign_all<C: Curve>(sks: &[Vec<u8>], pks: &[Vec<u8>], msg: &[u8], rands: &[[u8; 32]]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error> {
+    let ka = key_agg::<C>(pks)?;
+    let agg = C::agg_bytes(&ka.q);
+    let nonces: Vec<_> = (0..sks.len()).map(|i| nonce_gen::<C>(&rands[i], Some(&sks[i]), &pks[i], Some(&agg), Some(msg), None)).collect();
+    let pubnonces: Vec<Vec<u8>> = nonces.iter().map(|n| n.1.clone()).collect();
+    let aggnonce = nonce_agg::<C>(&pubnonces)?;
+    let mut psigs = Vec::new();
+    for (i, (sec, _)) in nonces.iter().enumerate() {
+        let p = sign::<C>(sec, &sks[i], &aggnonce, pks, msg)?;
+        if !partial_sig_verify::<C>(&p, &pubnonces, pks, msg, i)? {
+            return Err(Error::Value("partial signature does not verify"));
+        }
+        psigs.push(p);
+    }
+    partial_sig_agg::<C>(&psigs, &aggnonce, pks, msg)
+}

         0x0100 => webauthn_p256(key_data, message, sig),
@@ -30,6 +34,7 @@ pub fn verify(kind: u16, key_data: &[u8], message: &[u8; 32], sig: &[u8], pop: b
         0x0105 => schnorr::<ark_bls12_381::g1::Config>(key_data, message, sig, 64),
         0x0106 => bls12381_minsig(key_data, message, sig, pop),
         0x0107 => bls_bn254_minsig(key_data, message, sig, pop),

         _ => false,
     }
 }

 }

@@ -140,7 +145,7 @@ pub fn ed25519(pk: &[u8], message: &[u8], sig: &[u8]) -> bool {
 
 // --- BIP-340 ---

@@ -162,7 +167,7 @@ fn coord<F: PrimeField>(b: &[u8], w: usize) -> Option<F> {
