### Task 3: FROST verifiers: secp256k1 (RFC 9591) and bn254, DKG check, T-FROST

Order: after the vectors task (groups.json) and the registry stored-kinds task (M1); before the MuSig2 verifiers task (M2),
which imports `FrostSecp256k1.hashToScalar` / `FrostSecp256k1.DST_CHAL` from this task. No dependency on M2's `library Bip340`.

**Files:**
- Create: `smartcontracts/evm/src/ua/verifiers/UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier.sol`
- Create: `smartcontracts/evm/src/ua/verifiers/FrostBn254Verifier.sol`
- Create: `smartcontracts/evm/test/ua/UAFrost.t.sol`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `IUAPopVerifier` (`src/ua/IUAVerifier.sol`); `HashToCurve.expandMessageXmd`; `CurveSigs.schnorr`; `G1Bn254`
  (`src/zether/ZetherCurves.sol`); `SCHNORR_TAG` (`src/ua/verifiers/PairingVerifiers.sol`); `UAKinds.validateKeyData` /
  `UAKinds.UAKeyData` and `Bip340Verifier` (tests); `smartcontracts/vectors/ua/groups.json` (vectors task: `groups[]` kinds
  273/275, `dkg[]`, `pop`, `sigs`, `negatives`, `zfDkg`, `rfc9591E5`, `tFrost`) and `signatures.json` `.bip340Official`.
- Produces: `UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier` (0x0111) and `FrostBn254Verifier` (0x0113), both `IUAPopVerifier`:
  `verify(vk, ua_msg, sig)` on the stored group key (33 B / 64 B), `verifyPop(keyData, popMsg, sig)` = header/bounds +
  every DKG PoK (frost-core 3.0.0 challenge) + `Y == Σ C_i0` + the group signature; `library FrostSecp256k1`
  (`hashToScalar`, `DST_CHAL`, `DST_DKG`, `verify(bytes,bytes,bytes)`, `isLinear`, `decompress`). The registry wiring
  (verifier addresses, caps) is the M3 task's.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create the secp256k1 verifier** `smartcontracts/evm/src/ua/verifiers/UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier.sol`:
```solidity
// SPDX-License-Identifier: LicenseRef-SCaaS-Community-1.0
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import {IUAPopVerifier} from "../IUAVerifier.sol";
import {HashToCurve} from "../../common/HashToCurve.sol";

/// @title RFC 9591 FROST(secp256k1, SHA-256) verification and the frost-core DKG proof of knowledge (accounts.md §1.6.1).
/// @notice **UNAUDITED — NOT FOR PRODUCTION FUNDS.** Elements are SEC1 compressed (33), scalars 32-byte big-endian.
///         `H(m, label) = int(expand_message_xmd_SHA256(m, "FROST-secp256k1-SHA256-v1" ‖ label, 48)) mod N`
///         (RFC 9591 §6.5 `hash_to_field`; labels `chal` for H2, `dkg` for the ZF frost-core `HDKG`).
///         Every `z·G − c·Q == R` check is one `ecrecover(−z·x(Q), v(Q), x(Q), −c·x(Q))`, which returns
///         `address(x(Q)⁻¹·(−c·x(Q)·Q + z·x(Q)·G))`, compared with `address(R)` (R decompressed with one MODEXP).
///         Fails closed, never reverts: a point with prefix ∉ {02, 03}, `x ≥ p` or off the curve, `z ≥ N`, a failed
///         MODEXP (out of gas) ⇒ false. Not covered (probability ~2⁻¹²⁸ each, fail closed): `x(Q) ≥ N` (ecrecover
///         refuses r ≥ N) and `c == 0`.
library FrostSecp256k1 {
    uint256 internal constant P = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEFFFFFC2F;
    uint256 internal constant N = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEBAAEDCE6AF48A03BBFD25E8CD0364141;
    bytes internal constant DST_CHAL = "FROST-secp256k1-SHA256-v1chal";
    bytes internal constant DST_DKG = "FROST-secp256k1-SHA256-v1dkg";

    /// RFC 9591 `hash_to_field` to a scalar: 48 bytes of `expand_message_xmd` (SHA-256) mod N.
    function hashToScalar(bytes memory m, bytes memory dst) internal pure returns (uint256 s) {
        bytes memory u = HashToCurve.expandMessageXmd(m, dst, 48);
        uint256 hi;
        uint256 lo;
        assembly ("memory-safe") {
            hi := mload(add(u, 32))
            lo := shr(128, mload(add(u, 64)))
        }
        s = addmod(mulmod(hi, 1 << 128, N), lo, N);
    }

    /// RFC 9591 verify: `sig = R (33) ‖ z (32)`, `c = H2(R ‖ Y ‖ msg)`, accept iff `z < N` and `z·G == R + c·Y`.
    function verify(bytes memory y, bytes memory message, bytes memory sig) internal view returns (bool) {
        if (y.length != 33 || sig.length != 65) return false;
        (bool ok, uint256 rx, uint256 ry) = decompress(sig, 0);
        uint256 z = _word(sig, 33);
        if (!ok || z >= N) return false;
        uint256 c = hashToScalar(bytes.concat(_slice33(sig), y, message), DST_CHAL);
        return isLinear(y, 0, z, c, rx, ry);
    }

    /// `z·G − c·Q == (rx, ry)` with `Q` the compressed point at `q[o:o+33]` (lifted by ecrecover from its prefix).
    function isLinear(bytes memory q, uint256 o, uint256 z, uint256 c, uint256 rx, uint256 ry)
        internal pure returns (bool)
    {
        uint256 prefix = uint8(q[o]);
        uint256 qx = _word(q, o + 1);
        if (prefix != 2 && prefix != 3) return false;
        address got = ecrecover(bytes32(mulmod(N - z, qx, N)), uint8(prefix + 25), bytes32(qx), bytes32(mulmod(N - c, qx, N)));
        return got != address(0) && got == address(uint160(uint256(keccak256(abi.encodePacked(rx, ry)))));
    }

    /// SEC1 compressed point at `b[o:o+33]` → affine `(x, y)`; false on prefix ∉ {02, 03}, `x ≥ p`, off the curve.
    function decompress(bytes memory b, uint256 o) internal view returns (bool ok, uint256 x, uint256 y) {
        uint256 prefix = uint8(b[o]);
        x = _word(b, o + 1);
        if ((prefix != 2 && prefix != 3) || x >= P) return (false, 0, 0);
        uint256 rhs = addmod(mulmod(mulmod(x, x, P), x, P), 7, P);
        assembly ("memory-safe") {
            let m := mload(0x40)
            mstore(m, 32)
            mstore(add(m, 0x20), 32)
            mstore(add(m, 0x40), 32)
            mstore(add(m, 0x60), rhs)
            mstore(add(m, 0x80), 0x3fffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffbfffff0c) // (p + 1) / 4
            mstore(add(m, 0xa0), P)
            let success := staticcall(gas(), 0x05, m, 0xc0, m, 0x20)
            ok := and(success, eq(returndatasize(), 0x20)) // Yul evaluates arguments right to left
            y := mload(m)
        }
        if (!ok || mulmod(y, y, P) != rhs) return (false, 0, 0);
        if (y & 1 != prefix - 2) y = P - y;
    }

    function _word(bytes memory b, uint256 o) private pure returns (uint256 w) {
        assembly ("memory-safe") {
            w := mload(add(add(b, 32), o))
        }
    }

    function _slice33(bytes memory b) private pure returns (bytes memory s) {
        s = new bytes(33);
        assembly ("memory-safe") {
            mstore(add(s, 32), mload(add(b, 32)))
            mstore8(add(s, 64), byte(0, mload(add(b, 64))))
        }
    }
}

/// @title UA verifier for `frost-secp256k1` (0x0111, accounts.md §1.6.1, §1.9) — RFC 9591, **not** BIP-340.
/// @notice `verify(Y, ua_msg, R ‖ z)`: RFC 9591 FROST(secp256k1, SHA-256) over the 32-byte `ua_msg`, `Y` (33) the stored
///         group key. `verifyPop(keyData, msg, sig)`: key data = `Y (33) ‖ u16 t ‖ u16 n ‖ n × (t × C_ik (33) ‖ R_i (33)
///         ‖ μ_i (32))`, 2 ≤ t ≤ n ≤ 16, participant `i = 1..n` (RFC 9591 scalar identifiers); for each `i`:
///         `μ_i < N`, `R_i == μ_i·G − c_i·C_i0` with `c_i = H(i (32 BE) ‖ C_i0 ‖ R_i, "dkg")` (ZF frost-core
///         `keys::dkg` `challenge` / `verify_proof_of_knowledge`); `Y == Σ C_i0`; then `verify(Y, msg, sig)`.
///         `C_ik` for k ≥ 1 enter no equation on chain (Feldman share checks are each participant's duty, §1.6.1
///         attestation-only): their encoding is checked by `UAKinds.validateKeyData` at registration, not here.
///         Malformed input returns false, never reverts.
contract UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier is IUAPopVerifier {
    function verify(bytes calldata keyData, bytes32 message, bytes calldata sig) public view returns (bool) {
        return FrostSecp256k1.verify(keyData, abi.encodePacked(message), sig);
    }

    function verifyPop(bytes calldata keyData, bytes32 message, bytes calldata sig) external view returns (bool) {
        (bool ok, uint256[3] memory a) = _sumOfProven(keyData);
        if (!ok) return false;
        bytes memory y = keyData[0:33];
        (bool okY, uint256 yx, uint256 yy) = FrostSecp256k1.decompress(y, 0);
        uint256 p = FrostSecp256k1.P;
        uint256 z2 = mulmod(a[2], a[2], p);
        if (!okY || a[0] != mulmod(yx, z2, p) || a[1] != mulmod(yy, mulmod(z2, a[2], p), p)) return false;
        return FrostSecp256k1.verify(y, abi.encodePacked(message), sig);
    }

    /// Header and length checks, every PoK, and `Σ C_i0` in Jacobian coordinates (X, Y, Z) = affine (X/Z², Y/Z³).
    function _sumOfProven(bytes calldata keyData) private view returns (bool, uint256[3] memory a) {
        if (keyData.length < 37) return (false, a);
        uint256 t = uint16(bytes2(keyData[33:35]));
        uint256 n = uint16(bytes2(keyData[35:37]));
        if (t < 2 || t > n || n > 16) return (false, a);
        uint256 per = (t + 1) * 33 + 32;
        if (keyData.length != 37 + n * per) return (false, a);
        for (uint256 i; i < n; ++i) {
            (bool ok, uint256 cx, uint256 cy) = _pok(i + 1, keyData[37 + i * per:37 + (i + 1) * per], t);
            if (!ok) return (false, a);
            if (i == 0) (a[0], a[1], a[2]) = (cx, cy, 1);
            else if (!_addMixed(a, cx, cy)) return (false, a);
        }
        return (true, a);
    }

    /// Participant `id`'s record `part = t × C_ik ‖ R_i ‖ μ_i`: `μ_i < N`, `R_i == μ_i·G − c_i·C_i0`; returns affine `C_i0`.
    function _pok(uint256 id, bytes calldata part, uint256 t) private view returns (bool ok, uint256, uint256) {
        bytes memory c0 = part[0:33];
        bytes memory r = part[t * 33:t * 33 + 33];
        uint256 mu = uint256(bytes32(part[t * 33 + 33:]));
        uint256 rx;
        uint256 ry;
        (ok, rx, ry) = FrostSecp256k1.decompress(r, 0);
        if (!ok || mu >= FrostSecp256k1.N) return (false, 0, 0);
        uint256 ch = FrostSecp256k1.hashToScalar(abi.encodePacked(id, c0, r), FrostSecp256k1.DST_DKG);
        if (!FrostSecp256k1.isLinear(c0, 0, mu, ch, rx, ry)) return (false, 0, 0);
        return FrostSecp256k1.decompress(c0, 0);
    }

    /// `a += (x2, y2)`: Jacobian + affine addition (add-2007-bl, a = 0). False when the inputs share an x coordinate
    /// (doubling or a sum at infinity). ponytail: such a transcript is refused (an honest DKG hits it with probability
    /// ~2⁻²⁵⁶); add doubling only if a legitimate use for repeated or cancelling C_i0 appears.
    function _addMixed(uint256[3] memory a, uint256 x2, uint256 y2) private pure returns (bool) {
        uint256 p = FrostSecp256k1.P;
        (uint256 x1, uint256 y1, uint256 z1) = (a[0], a[1], a[2]);
        uint256 zz = mulmod(z1, z1, p);
        uint256 h = addmod(mulmod(x2, zz, p), p - x1, p);
        if (h == 0) return false;
        uint256 r = addmod(mulmod(y2, mulmod(zz, z1, p), p), p - y1, p);
        uint256 hh = mulmod(h, h, p);
        uint256 hhh = mulmod(hh, h, p);
        uint256 v = mulmod(x1, hh, p);
        uint256 x3 = addmod(addmod(mulmod(r, r, p), p - hhh, p), p - addmod(v, v, p), p);
        a[1] = addmod(mulmod(r, addmod(v, p - x3, p), p), p - mulmod(y1, hhh, p), p);
        a[0] = x3;
        a[2] = mulmod(z1, h, p);
        return true;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Create the bn254 verifier** `smartcontracts/evm/src/ua/verifiers/FrostBn254Verifier.sol`:
```solidity
// SPDX-License-Identifier: LicenseRef-SCaaS-Community-1.0
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import {IUAPopVerifier} from "../IUAVerifier.sol";
import {CurveSigs} from "../../common/CurveSigs.sol";
import {G1Bn254} from "../../zether/ZetherCurves.sol";
import {SCHNORR_TAG} from "./PairingVerifiers.sol";

/// @title UA verifier for `frost-bn254` (0x0113, accounts.md §1.6.1, §1.9): FROST(bn254, keccak256).
/// @notice **UNAUDITED — NOT FOR PRODUCTION FUNDS.** `verify(Y, ua_msg, R ‖ z)`: the `schnorr-bn254` check
///         (`CurveSigs.schnorr` under `"scaas.ua.schnorr.v1"`; the ciphersuite's H2 is that challenge), `Y` (64) the
///         stored group key. `verifyPop(keyData, msg, sig)`: key data = `Y (64) ‖ u16 t ‖ u16 n ‖ n × (t × C_ik (64) ‖
///         R_i (64) ‖ μ_i (32))`, 2 ≤ t ≤ n ≤ 16, participant `i = 1..n`; for each `i`: `C_i0`, `R_i` canonical and on
///         the curve (checked in Solidity, so no precompile can fail on them), `μ_i < r`,
///         `[μ_i]G + [r − c_i]C_i0 == R_i` with `c_i = H_dkg(i (32 BE) ‖ C_i0 ‖ R_i)`,
///         `H_dkg(m) = int(keccak256(ctx ‖ "dkg" ‖ m ‖ 0x00) ‖ keccak256(ctx ‖ "dkg" ‖ m ‖ 0x01)) mod r`,
///         `ctx = "FROST-BN254-KECCAK256-SCAAS-v1"`; `Y == Σ C_i0`; then `verify(Y, msg, sig)`. `C_ik` for k ≥ 1 are
///         checked by `UAKinds.validateKeyData` at registration, not here (see `UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier`).
///         Malformed input returns false; only a caller that gives too little gas for the precompiles sees a revert.
contract FrostBn254Verifier is IUAPopVerifier {
    bytes internal constant DKG_PREFIX = "FROST-BN254-KECCAK256-SCAAS-v1dkg";
    uint256 private constant TWO_256_MOD_R = (type(uint256).max % G1Bn254.R) + 1;

    function verify(bytes calldata keyData, bytes32 message, bytes calldata sig) external view returns (bool) {
        return keyData.length == 64 && CurveSigs.schnorr(SCHNORR_TAG, keyData, message, sig);
    }

    function verifyPop(bytes calldata keyData, bytes32 message, bytes calldata sig) external view returns (bool) {
        if (keyData.length < 68) return false;
        uint256 t = uint16(bytes2(keyData[64:66]));
        uint256 n = uint16(bytes2(keyData[66:68]));
        if (t < 2 || t > n || n > 16) return false;
        uint256 per = (t + 1) * 64 + 32;
        if (keyData.length != 68 + n * per) return false;
        bytes memory acc = G1Bn254.identity();
        for (uint256 i; i < n; ++i) {
            uint256 o = 68 + i * per;
            bytes memory c0 = keyData[o:o + 64];
            if (!_pok(i + 1, c0, keyData[o + t * 64:o + per])) return false;
            acc = G1Bn254.add(acc, c0);
        }
        bytes memory y = keyData[0:64];
        if (keccak256(acc) != keccak256(y)) return false;
        return CurveSigs.schnorr(SCHNORR_TAG, y, message, sig);
    }

    /// Participant `id`'s PoK: `rm = R_i (64) ‖ μ_i (32)`; `[μ_i]G + [r − c_i]C_i0 == R_i`.
    function _pok(uint256 id, bytes memory c0, bytes calldata rm) private view returns (bool) {
        bytes memory rPt = rm[0:64];
        uint256 mu = uint256(bytes32(rm[64:96]));
        uint256 r = G1Bn254.R;
        if (!_onCurve(c0) || !_onCurve(rPt) || mu >= r) return false;
        uint256 hi = uint256(keccak256(abi.encodePacked(DKG_PREFIX, id, c0, rPt, uint8(0))));
        uint256 lo = uint256(keccak256(abi.encodePacked(DKG_PREFIX, id, c0, rPt, uint8(1))));
        uint256[] memory sc = new uint256[](2);
        (sc[0], sc[1]) = (mu, r - addmod(mulmod(hi, TWO_256_MOD_R, r), lo, r));
        return keccak256(G1Bn254.msm(bytes.concat(G1Bn254.generator(), c0), sc)) == keccak256(rPt);
    }

    /// 64 bytes, canonical, on `y² = x³ + 3` (excludes O = (0, 0)).
    function _onCurve(bytes memory a) private pure returns (bool) {
        (uint256 x, uint256 y) = abi.decode(a, (uint256, uint256));
        uint256 q = G1Bn254.P;
        return x < q && y < q && mulmod(y, y, q) == addmod(mulmod(mulmod(x, x, q), x, q), 3, q);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create the tests** `smartcontracts/evm/test/ua/UAFrost.t.sol`:
```solidity
// SPDX-License-Identifier: LicenseRef-SCaaS-Community-1.0
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import {Test, console} from "forge-std/Test.sol";
import {HashToCurve} from "../../src/common/HashToCurve.sol";
import {UAKinds} from "../../src/ua/UAKinds.sol";
import {Bip340Verifier} from "../../src/ua/verifiers/Bip340Verifier.sol";
import {FrostSecp256k1, UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier} from "../../src/ua/verifiers/UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier.sol";
import {FrostBn254Verifier} from "../../src/ua/verifiers/FrostBn254Verifier.sol";

/// RFC 9591 verify over an arbitrary-length message (the RFC vectors sign "test", the UA kinds sign 32 bytes).
contract FrostHarness {
    function verifyBytes(bytes memory y, bytes memory m, bytes memory sig) external view returns (bool) {
        return FrostSecp256k1.verify(y, m, sig);
    }
}

contract KindsHarness {
    function validate(uint16 kind, bytes memory d) external view {
        UAKinds.validateKeyData(kind, d, false);
    }
}

/// `UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier` and `FrostBn254Verifier` (accounts.md §1.6.1, §1.9) on smartcontracts/vectors/ua/groups.json:
/// every FROST group (PoP via `verifyPop`, stored-key `verify`), the DKG rows' `H_dkg` preimages and challenges, `zfDkg`,
/// RFC 9591 Appendix E.5, T-FROST (`tFrost[]`), the main groups' sigs and negatives; plus RFC 9380 K.1, extra DKG
/// negatives, malformed input never reverts, gas (`-vv`).
contract UAFrostTest is Test {
    string internal gj;
    string internal sj;
    UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier internal fs;
    FrostBn254Verifier internal fb;
    Bip340Verifier internal bip;
    FrostHarness internal h;
    KindsHarness internal kh;
    uint256[] internal frostGroups; // indexes into groups[] of kinds 0x0111 / 0x0113
    uint256 internal sMain;
    uint256 internal bMain;

    uint16 internal constant FROST_SECP = 0x0111;
    uint16 internal constant FROST_BN = 0x0113;
    uint256 internal constant SECP_P = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEFFFFFC2F;
    uint256 internal constant SECP_N = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEBAAEDCE6AF48A03BBFD25E8CD0364141;
    uint256 internal constant BN_P = 21888242871839275222246405745257275088696311157297823662689037894645226208583;
    uint256 internal constant BN_R = 21888242871839275222246405745257275088548364400416034343698204186575808495617;

    /// ZF frost-secp256k1 3.0.0 `tests/helpers/vectors.json`: E.5 key and message, other nonce randomness than the RFC text.
    bytes internal constant E5_SIG_ZF =
        hex"024c1ad4e031872661fa6ebd05dfc7fb30db08b38d79f0edbc82051ae931381bc6a46881e25c7989d3816eae32074f1ab0d49ee908a59713ed5284c6bade7cfb02";
    /// `zfDkg` has no PoP: an RFC 9591 signature over ZF_MSG = keccak256("ua2c frost fixture message") by
    /// `s = Σ signing_key` of `vectors_dkg.json` (Σ s_i·G = its verifying_key), made once with a Python RFC 9591 signer.
    bytes32 internal constant ZF_MSG = 0xe1352934f280ed376861f0a7e09acc380153101c3871257792f04d8fc208abbe;
    bytes internal constant ZF_SIG =
        hex"03f580a2e6d16da374db56831869718cd069d2ca1e84006b901cb370de1927cd08916c42b72a61c11b026fc5e0f5714a9d53a30170a15321c6f5e9ae10ad30961a";

    function setUp() public {
        gj = vm.readFile("../vectors/ua/groups.json");
        sj = vm.readFile("../vectors/ua/signatures.json");
        fs = new UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier();
        fb = new FrostBn254Verifier();
        bip = new Bip340Verifier();
        h = new FrostHarness();
        kh = new KindsHarness();
        for (uint256 i; vm.keyExistsJson(gj, string.concat(".groups[", vm.toString(i), "]")); ++i) {
            uint256 kind = vm.parseJsonUint(gj, _g(i, "kind"));
            if (kind != FROST_SECP && kind != FROST_BN) continue;
            frostGroups.push(i);
            if (keccak256(bytes(vm.parseJsonString(gj, _g(i, "role")))) == keccak256("main")) {
                if (kind == FROST_SECP) sMain = i;
                else bMain = i;
            }
        }
    }

    function _g(uint256 i, string memory f) internal pure returns (string memory) {
        return string.concat(".groups[", vm.toString(i), "].", f);
    }

    function _gb(uint256 i, string memory f) internal view returns (bytes memory) {
        return vm.parseJsonBytes(gj, _g(i, f));
    }

    function _secp(uint256 i) internal view returns (bool) {
        return vm.parseJsonUint(gj, _g(i, "kind")) == FROST_SECP;
    }

    // ---- RFC 9380 expand_message_xmd (SHA-256), Appendix K.1, through the library the FROST hashes use

    function test_xmd_rfc9380_k1() public pure {
        bytes memory dst = "QUUX-V01-CS02-with-expander-SHA256-128";
        assertEq(HashToCurve.expandMessageXmd("", dst, 0x20), hex"68a985b87eb6b46952128911f2a4412bbc302a9d759667f87f7a21d803f07235");
        assertEq(HashToCurve.expandMessageXmd("abc", dst, 0x20), hex"d8ccab23b5985ccea865c6c97b6e5b8350e794e603b4b97902f53a8a0d605615");
        assertEq(
            HashToCurve.expandMessageXmd("abcdef0123456789", dst, 0x20),
            hex"eff31487c770a893cfb36f912fbfcbff40d5661771ca4b2cb4eafe524333f5c1"
        );
        assertEq(
            HashToCurve.expandMessageXmd("", dst, 0x80),
            hex"af84c27ccfd45d41914fdff5df25293e221afc53d8ad2ac06d5e3e29485dadbee0d121587713a3e0dd4d5e69e93eb7cd4f5df4"
            hex"cd103e188cf60cb02edc3edf18eda8576c412b18ffb658e3dd6ec849469b979d444cf7b26911a08e63cf31f9dcc541708d3491184472"
            hex"c2c29bb749d4286b004ceb5ee6b9a7fa5b646c993f0ced"
        );
        assertEq(
            HashToCurve.expandMessageXmd("abc", dst, 0x80),
            hex"abba86a6129e366fc877aab32fc4ffc70120d8996c88aee2fe4b32d6c7b6437a647e6c3163d40b76a73cf6a5674ef1d890f95b"
            hex"664ee0afa5359a5c4e07985635bbecbac65d747d3d2da7ec2b8221b17b0ca9dc8a1ac1c07ea6a1e60583e2cb00058e77b7b72a298425"
            hex"cd1b941ad4ec65e8afc50303a22c0f99b0509b4c895f40"
        );
    }

    // ---- RFC 9591 Appendix E.5 FROST(secp256k1, SHA-256)

    function test_rfc9591_e5() public view {
        bytes memory y = vm.parseJsonBytes(gj, ".rfc9591E5.groupPublicKey");
        bytes memory m = vm.parseJsonBytes(gj, ".rfc9591E5.message");
        bytes memory sig = vm.parseJsonBytes(gj, ".rfc9591E5.sig");
        assertEq(y, hex"02f37c34b66ced1fb51c34a90bdae006901f10625cc06c4f64663b0eae87d87b4f");
        assertEq(m, "test");
        assertEq(sig, hex"0205b6d04d3774c8929413e3c76024d54149c372d57aae62574ed74319b5ea14d0c65dde8492a7471437e6c2fe3da49b90d23f642b5c6dbe7e36089f096dd97324");
        assertTrue(h.verifyBytes(y, m, sig), "RFC text sig");
        assertTrue(h.verifyBytes(y, m, E5_SIG_ZF), "ZF vectors.json sig");
        assertFalse(h.verifyBytes(y, "tesu", sig), "other message");
        assertFalse(h.verifyBytes(hex"03f37c34b66ced1fb51c34a90bdae006901f10625cc06c4f64663b0eae87d87b4f", m, sig), "-Y");
        for (uint256 k; k < 65; k += 8) {
            bytes memory bad = bytes.concat(sig);
            bad[k] ^= 0x01;
            assertFalse(h.verifyBytes(y, m, bad), "mutated sig");
        }
        assertFalse(h.verifyBytes(y, m, bytes.concat(_slice(sig, 0, 33), bytes32(SECP_N))), "z = N");
        assertFalse(h.verifyBytes(y, m, _slice(sig, 0, 64)), "short sig");
    }

    // ---- T-FROST: RFC 9591 signatures are not BIP-340 signatures and vice versa

    /// `tFrost[]`: 10 rows. The BIP-340 re-encoding of E.5 signs the 4-byte "test", which `Bip340Verifier` (32-byte
    /// `ua_msg` only) cannot take: that one row is skipped and counted.
    function test_tFrost() public view {
        uint256 used;
        uint256 skipped;
        for (uint256 i; vm.keyExistsJson(gj, string.concat(".tFrost[", vm.toString(i), "]")); ++i) {
            string memory p = string.concat(".tFrost[", vm.toString(i), "].");
            bytes memory key = vm.parseJsonBytes(gj, string.concat(p, "key"));
            bytes memory m = vm.parseJsonBytes(gj, string.concat(p, "message"));
            bytes memory sig = vm.parseJsonBytes(gj, string.concat(p, "sig"));
            bool valid = vm.parseJsonBool(gj, string.concat(p, "valid"));
            bool got;
            if (keccak256(bytes(vm.parseJsonString(gj, string.concat(p, "verifier")))) == keccak256("frost-secp256k1")) {
                got = m.length == 32 ? fs.verify(key, bytes32(m), sig) : h.verifyBytes(key, m, sig);
            } else {
                if (m.length != 32) {
                    assertFalse(valid);
                    ++skipped;
                    continue;
                }
                got = bip.verify(key, bytes32(m), sig);
            }
            assertEq(got, valid, vm.parseJsonString(gj, string.concat(p, "name")));
            ++used;
        }
        assertEq(used, 9);
        assertEq(skipped, 1);
    }

    function test_tfrost_bip340Official() public view {
        uint256 n;
        for (uint256 i; vm.keyExistsJson(sj, string.concat(".bip340Official[", vm.toString(i), "]")); ++i) {
            string memory p = string.concat(".bip340Official[", vm.toString(i), "]");
            if (!vm.parseJsonBool(sj, string.concat(p, ".valid"))) continue;
            bytes memory m = vm.parseJsonBytes(sj, string.concat(p, ".message"));
            if (m.length != 32) continue;
            bytes memory pk = vm.parseJsonBytes(sj, string.concat(p, ".publicKey"));
            bytes memory sig = vm.parseJsonBytes(sj, string.concat(p, ".sig"));
            assertTrue(bip.verify(pk, bytes32(m), sig));
            assertFalse(fs.verify(bytes.concat(hex"02", pk), bytes32(m), bytes.concat(hex"02", sig)));
            ++n;
        }
        assertGe(n, 3, "official BIP-340 rows used");
    }

    // ---- groups.json FROST groups: PoP (verifyPop), stored key (verify), sigs, DKG rows, zfDkg

    function test_groups() public view {
        assertEq(frostGroups.length, 12);
        uint256[2][6] memory tn = [[uint256(2), 3], [uint256(2), 2], [uint256(2), 8], [uint256(8), 8], [uint256(2), 16], [uint256(16), 16]];
        for (uint256 j; j < 12; ++j) {
            uint256 i = frostGroups[j];
            bool secp = _secp(i);
            assertEq(secp, j < 6);
            assertEq(vm.parseJsonUint(gj, _g(i, "t")), tn[j % 6][0]);
            assertEq(vm.parseJsonUint(gj, _g(i, "n")), tn[j % 6][1]);
            bytes memory kd = _gb(i, "keyData");
            bytes memory vk = _gb(i, "vk");
            bytes32 m = vm.parseJsonBytes32(gj, _g(i, "pop.message"));
            bytes memory blob = _gb(i, "pop.blob");
            assertEq(vk, _slice(kd, 0, secp ? 33 : 64));
            assertEq(blob, _gb(i, "pop.sig"));
            assertTrue(_pop(secp, kd, m, blob), vm.parseJsonString(gj, _g(i, "name")));
            assertTrue(_verify(secp, vk, m, blob));
            assertFalse(_pop(secp, kd, bytes32(uint256(m) ^ 1), blob), "other message");
        }
        assertEq(sMain, frostGroups[0]);
        assertEq(bMain, frostGroups[6]);
    }

    function test_mainSigs() public view {
        uint256[2] memory mains = [sMain, bMain];
        uint256 n;
        for (uint256 k; k < 2; ++k) {
            uint256 i = mains[k];
            bytes memory vk = _gb(i, "vk");
            for (uint256 s; vm.keyExistsJson(gj, _g(i, string.concat("sigs[", vm.toString(s), "]"))); ++s) {
                string memory p = string.concat("sigs[", vm.toString(s), "].");
                bytes32 m = vm.parseJsonBytes32(gj, _g(i, string.concat(p, "message")));
                assertTrue(_verify(k == 0, vk, m, _gb(i, string.concat(p, "sig"))));
                ++n;
            }
        }
        assertEq(n, 4);
    }

    /// A `dkg[]` row as `vm.parseJson` decodes it (fields in alphabetical order; 32-byte hex strings become bytes32).
    struct DkgRow {
        bytes R;
        bytes32 challenge;
        bytes[] commitments;
        uint256 id;
        bytes32 mu;
        bytes preimage;
    }

    /// Each `dkg[]` row: `preimage == id (32 BE) ‖ C_i0 ‖ R_i`, `challenge == H_dkg(preimage)` (secp256k1 through the
    /// verifier's library; bn254 recomputed here per the ciphersuite table), and the row equals its key-data slice.
    function test_dkgRows() public view {
        uint256 rows;
        for (uint256 j; j < 12; ++j) rows += this.dkgGroup(frostGroups[j]);
        assertEq(rows, 106);
    }

    /// One group per external frame (memory of the parsed JSON is released between groups).
    function dkgGroup(uint256 i) external view returns (uint256) {
        string memory json = vm.readFile("../vectors/ua/groups.json");
        bool secp = vm.parseJsonUint(json, _g(i, "kind")) == FROST_SECP;
        bytes memory kd = vm.parseJsonBytes(json, _g(i, "keyData"));
        DkgRow[] memory rows = abi.decode(vm.parseJson(json, _g(i, "dkg")), (DkgRow[]));
        (uint256 size, uint256 t, uint256 per, uint256 base) = _layout(kd, secp);
        for (uint256 p; p < rows.length; ++p) {
            DkgRow memory r = rows[p];
            uint256 o = base + p * per;
            assertEq(r.id, p + 1);
            assertEq(r.commitments.length, t);
            for (uint256 k; k < t; ++k) assertEq(r.commitments[k], _slice(kd, o + k * size, size));
            assertEq(r.R, _slice(kd, o + t * size, size));
            assertEq(r.mu, bytes32(_word(kd, o + (t + 1) * size)));
            assertEq(r.preimage, abi.encodePacked(p + 1, r.commitments[0], r.R));
            uint256 c = secp ? FrostSecp256k1.hashToScalar(r.preimage, FrostSecp256k1.DST_DKG) : _hdkgBn(r.preimage);
            assertEq(bytes32(c), r.challenge);
        }
        return rows.length;
    }

    function _hdkgBn(bytes memory m) internal pure returns (uint256) {
        bytes memory pre = bytes.concat("FROST-BN254-KECCAK256-SCAAS-v1", "dkg", m);
        uint256 hi = uint256(keccak256(bytes.concat(pre, hex"00")));
        uint256 lo = uint256(keccak256(bytes.concat(pre, hex"01")));
        return addmod(mulmod(hi, (type(uint256).max % BN_R) + 1, BN_R), lo, BN_R);
    }

    /// The ZF-authored DKG transcript passes the on-chain DKG check (with the inline group signature).
    function test_zfDkg() public view {
        bytes memory kd = vm.parseJsonBytes(gj, ".zfDkg.keyData");
        assertTrue(fs.verifyPop(kd, ZF_MSG, ZF_SIG));
        (, uint256 t,, uint256 base) = _layout(kd, true);
        bytes memory bad = bytes.concat(kd);
        _put(bad, base + (t + 1) * 33, _word(kd, base + (t + 1) * 33) ^ 1);
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(bad, ZF_MSG, ZF_SIG), "mu_1 mutated");
    }

    /// Main groups' `negatives[]`: `register` rows — `verifyPop` false; `UAKeyData` rows are refused by
    /// `UAKinds.validateKeyData`, `UAPoP` rows pass it; `sigValid` ⇒ the signature alone verifies against keyData's Y
    /// (only the group check refuses). `verify` rows — false.
    function test_negatives() public view {
        uint256[2] memory mains = [sMain, bMain];
        uint256[2] memory counts;
        for (uint256 k; k < 2; ++k) {
            uint256 i = mains[k];
            bool secp = k == 0;
            uint16 kind = secp ? FROST_SECP : FROST_BN;
            for (uint256 s; vm.keyExistsJson(gj, _g(i, string.concat("negatives[", vm.toString(s), "]"))); ++s) {
                string memory p = string.concat("negatives[", vm.toString(s), "].");
                string memory name = vm.parseJsonString(gj, _g(i, string.concat(p, "name")));
                bytes32 err = keccak256(bytes(vm.parseJsonString(gj, _g(i, string.concat(p, "error")))));
                bytes32 m = vm.parseJsonBytes32(gj, _g(i, string.concat(p, "message")));
                if (keccak256(bytes(vm.parseJsonString(gj, _g(i, string.concat(p, "entry"))))) == keccak256("verify")) {
                    assertEq(err, keccak256("false"));
                    assertFalse(_verify(secp, _gb(i, string.concat(p, "vk")), m, _gb(i, string.concat(p, "sig"))), name);
                } else {
                    bytes memory kd = _gb(i, string.concat(p, "keyData"));
                    bytes memory pop = _gb(i, string.concat(p, "pop"));
                    assertFalse(_pop(secp, kd, m, pop), name);
                    if (err == keccak256("UAKeyData")) {
                        try kh.validate(kind, kd) {
                            revert(string.concat(name, ": validateKeyData accepted"));
                        } catch (bytes memory e) {
                            assertEq(e, abi.encodeWithSelector(UAKinds.UAKeyData.selector, kind), name);
                        }
                    } else {
                        assertEq(err, keccak256("UAPoP"), name);
                        kh.validate(kind, kd);
                    }
                    if (vm.parseJsonBool(gj, _g(i, string.concat(p, "sigValid")))) {
                        assertTrue(_verify(secp, _slice(kd, 0, secp ? 33 : 64), m, pop), string.concat(name, " sigValid"));
                    }
                }
                ++counts[k];
            }
        }
        assertEq(counts[0], 16);
        assertEq(counts[1], 14);
    }

    // ---- DKG negatives not in groups.json (main groups)

    /// μ_i = order (non-canonical) and R_1 replaced by R_2, per curve.
    function test_badPoK_extra() public view {
        for (uint256 k; k < 2; ++k) {
            (bool secp, bytes memory kd, bytes32 m, bytes memory sig) = _main(k == 0);
            (uint256 size, uint256 t, uint256 per, uint256 base) = _layout(kd, secp);
            for (uint256 i; i < 3; ++i) {
                bytes memory bad = bytes.concat(kd);
                _put(bad, base + i * per + (t + 1) * size, secp ? SECP_N : BN_R);
                assertFalse(_pop(secp, bad, m, sig), "mu = order");
            }
            bytes memory swapR = bytes.concat(kd);
            _copy(swapR, base + t * size, kd, base + per + t * size, size);
            assertFalse(_pop(secp, swapR, m, sig), "R_1 := R_2");
            assertTrue(_pop(secp, kd, m, sig));
        }
    }

    /// Non-canonical / off-curve Y, C_i0, R_i.
    function test_badPoints_secp() public view {
        (, bytes memory kd, bytes32 m, bytes memory sig) = _main(true);
        (, uint256 t, uint256 per, uint256 base) = _layout(kd, true);
        uint256[3] memory offs = [uint256(0), base + per, base + per + t * 33]; // Y, C_20, R_2
        for (uint256 k; k < 3; ++k) {
            uint256 o = offs[k];
            bytes memory bad = bytes.concat(kd);
            bad[o] = 0x04;
            assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(bad, m, sig), "prefix 04");
            bad[o] = 0x00;
            assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(bad, m, sig), "prefix 00");
            bad = bytes.concat(kd);
            _put(bad, o + 1, SECP_P + 1); // x >= p (x = p + 1 ≡ 1, a valid x mod p)
            assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(bad, m, sig), "x >= p");
            _put(bad, o + 1, 5); // x³ + 7 is a non-residue
            assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(bad, m, sig), "off curve");
        }
    }

    function test_badPoints_bn() public view {
        (, bytes memory kd, bytes32 m, bytes memory sig) = _main(false);
        (, uint256 t, uint256 per, uint256 base) = _layout(kd, false);
        uint256[3] memory boffs = [uint256(0), base + per, base + per + t * 64];
        for (uint256 k; k < 3; ++k) {
            uint256 o = boffs[k];
            bytes memory bad = bytes.concat(kd);
            _put(bad, o + 32, _word(kd, o + 32) + BN_P); // y + p: non-canonical
            assertFalse(fb.verifyPop(bad, m, sig), "bn y + p");
            bad = bytes.concat(kd);
            _put(bad, o + 32, addmod(_word(kd, o + 32), 1, BN_P));
            assertFalse(fb.verifyPop(bad, m, sig), "bn off curve");
            bad = bytes.concat(kd);
            _put(bad, o, 0);
            _put(bad, o + 32, 0);
            assertFalse(fb.verifyPop(bad, m, sig), "bn identity");
        }
    }

    /// n / t bounds and truncation; `verifyPop` refuses before touching a point.
    function test_bounds() public view {
        (, bytes memory kd, bytes32 m, bytes memory sig) = _main(true);
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(_setU16(kd, 35, 17), m, sig), "n = 17");
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(_setU16(kd, 35, 1), m, sig), "t > n");
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(_setU16(kd, 35, 0), m, sig), "n = 0");
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(_setU16(kd, 33, 0), m, sig), "t = 0");
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(_slice(kd, 0, kd.length - 1), m, sig), "truncated");
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(bytes.concat(kd, hex"00"), m, sig), "trailing byte");
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(_slice(kd, 0, 36), m, sig), "short header");
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop("", m, sig), "empty");
        assertFalse(fs.verify(_slice(kd, 0, 32), m, sig), "vk 32 bytes");
        assertFalse(fs.verify(kd, m, sig), "vk = key data");
        uint256 big = frostGroups[5]; // t = n = 16: right length for 16, header says 17
        assertFalse(fs.verifyPop(_setU16(_setU16(_gb(big, "keyData"), 33, 17), 35, 17), m, _gb(big, "pop.blob")), "t = n = 17");
        (, kd, m, sig) = _main(false);
        assertFalse(fb.verifyPop(_setU16(kd, 66, 17), m, sig), "bn n = 17");
        assertFalse(fb.verifyPop(_setU16(kd, 64, 0), m, sig), "bn t = 0");
        assertFalse(fb.verifyPop(_setU16(kd, 66, 1), m, sig), "bn t > n");
        assertFalse(fb.verifyPop(_slice(kd, 0, kd.length - 1), m, sig), "bn truncated");
        assertFalse(fb.verifyPop(_slice(kd, 0, 67), m, sig), "bn short header");
        assertFalse(fb.verifyPop("", m, sig), "bn empty");
        assertFalse(fb.verify(_slice(kd, 0, 63), m, sig), "bn vk 63 bytes");
        assertFalse(fb.verify(kd, m, sig), "bn vk = key data");
    }

    // ---- malformed input never reverts (a revert fails the test); mutations outside C_ik (k ≥ 1) answer false

    function testFuzz_randomNeverReverts(bytes calldata a, bytes calldata b, bytes32 m) public view {
        fs.verify(a, m, b);
        fs.verifyPop(a, m, b);
        fb.verify(a, m, b);
        fb.verifyPop(a, m, b);
    }

    function testFuzz_mutatedSecp(uint256 pos, uint8 delta) public view {
        _mutate(true, pos, delta);
    }

    function testFuzz_mutatedBn(uint256 pos, uint8 delta) public view {
        _mutate(false, pos, delta);
    }

    function _mutate(bool secp_, uint256 pos, uint8 delta) internal view {
        (bool secp, bytes memory kd, bytes32 m, bytes memory sig) = _main(secp_);
        delta = uint8(bound(delta, 1, 255));
        pos = bound(pos, 0, kd.length + sig.length - 1);
        (uint256 size, uint256 t, uint256 per, uint256 base) = _layout(kd, secp);
        bool higher;
        if (pos < kd.length) {
            kd[pos] = bytes1(uint8(kd[pos]) ^ delta);
            if (pos >= base) {
                uint256 inPart = (pos - base) % per;
                higher = inPart >= size && inPart < t * size; // C_ik, k ≥ 1: not an input of any equation
            }
        } else {
            sig[pos - kd.length] = bytes1(uint8(sig[pos - kd.length]) ^ delta);
        }
        bool ok = _pop(secp, kd, m, sig);
        if (!higher) assertFalse(ok);
    }

    // ---- gas (T-GAS input): verify on the stored key and verifyPop, every groups.json FROST group, warm verifiers

    function test_gas() public view {
        fs.verify("", 0, "");
        fb.verify("", 0, "");
        uint256[6] memory capPop = [uint256(100_000), 80_000, 200_000, 220_000, 380_000, 420_000];
        for (uint256 j; j < 12; ++j) {
            uint256 i = frostGroups[j];
            bool secp = _secp(i);
            bytes memory vk = _gb(i, "vk");
            bytes memory kd = _gb(i, "keyData");
            bytes32 m = vm.parseJsonBytes32(gj, _g(i, "pop.message"));
            bytes memory sig = _gb(i, "pop.blob");
            uint256 g = gasleft();
            bool ok = _verify(secp, vk, m, sig);
            uint256 gv = g - gasleft();
            assertTrue(ok);
            g = gasleft();
            ok = _pop(secp, kd, m, sig);
            uint256 gp = g - gasleft();
            assertTrue(ok);
            console.log(vm.parseJsonString(gj, _g(i, "kindName")), vm.parseJsonString(gj, _g(i, "name")));
            console.log("  verify / verifyPop", gv, gp);
            assertLt(gv, secp ? 21_000 : 32_000);
            assertLt(gp, capPop[j % 6]);
        }
    }

    // ---- helpers

    function _main(bool secp) internal view returns (bool, bytes memory kd, bytes32 m, bytes memory sig) {
        uint256 i = secp ? sMain : bMain;
        return (secp, _gb(i, "keyData"), vm.parseJsonBytes32(gj, _g(i, "pop.message")), _gb(i, "pop.blob"));
    }

    function _pop(bool secp, bytes memory kd, bytes32 m, bytes memory sig) internal view returns (bool) {
        return secp ? fs.verifyPop(kd, m, sig) : fb.verifyPop(kd, m, sig);
    }

    function _verify(bool secp, bytes memory vk, bytes32 m, bytes memory sig) internal view returns (bool) {
        return secp ? fs.verify(vk, m, sig) : fb.verify(vk, m, sig);
    }

    function _layout(bytes memory kd, bool secp) internal pure returns (uint256 size, uint256 t, uint256 per, uint256 base) {
        size = secp ? 33 : 64;
        t = (uint256(uint8(kd[size])) << 8) | uint8(kd[size + 1]);
        per = (t + 1) * size + 32;
        base = size + 4;
    }

    function _slice(bytes memory b, uint256 o, uint256 len) internal pure returns (bytes memory s) {
        s = new bytes(len);
        for (uint256 i; i < len; ++i) s[i] = b[o + i];
    }

    function _copy(bytes memory dst, uint256 dOff, bytes memory src, uint256 sOff, uint256 len) internal pure {
        for (uint256 i; i < len; ++i) dst[dOff + i] = src[sOff + i];
    }

    function _word(bytes memory b, uint256 o) internal pure returns (uint256 w) {
        assembly {
            w := mload(add(add(b, 32), o))
        }
    }

    function _put(bytes memory b, uint256 o, uint256 w) internal pure {
        assembly {
            mstore(add(add(b, 32), o), w)
        }
    }

    function _setU16(bytes memory b, uint256 o, uint256 v) internal pure returns (bytes memory c) {
        c = bytes.concat(b);
        c[o] = bytes1(uint8(v >> 8));
        c[o + 1] = bytes1(uint8(v));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: BIP-340 library.** Create `smartcontracts/evm/src/ua/verifiers/Bip340.sol` with exactly this content:

```solidity
// SPDX-License-Identifier: LicenseRef-SCaaS-Community-1.0
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

/// @title BIP-340 verify on the EVM (accounts.md §1.9 `bip340` / `musig2-secp256k1` row), shared by `Bip340Verifier`
///        and `Musig2Secp256k1Verifier`.
/// @notice `e = int(sha256(T ‖ T ‖ r ‖ P ‖ m)) mod n` with `T = sha256("BIP0340/challenge")`, accept iff
///         `R = s·G − e·P` is not infinity, has even y and `x(R) == r`. EVM: `ecrecover(−s·P_x, 27, P_x, −e·P_x)`
///         returns `address(P_x⁻¹·(−e·P_x·P + s·P_x·G)) = address(R)` (v = 27 selects P's even-y lift); the expected
///         `R` is `r` lifted to even y with one MODEXP (`p ≡ 3 mod 4`). Off-curve `P` or `r`, `P ≥ p`, `r ≥ p`, `s ≥ n`
///         and a wrong signature length return false. Not covered (probability ~2⁻¹²⁸ each, fail closed): `P_x ≥ n`
///         (ecrecover refuses r ≥ n) and `e == 0`. **UNAUDITED — NOT FOR PRODUCTION FUNDS.**
library Bip340 {
    uint256 internal constant P = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEFFFFFC2F;
    uint256 internal constant N = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEBAAEDCE6AF48A03BBFD25E8CD0364141;
    /// sha256("BIP0340/challenge"), pinned by the tests.
    bytes32 internal constant CHALLENGE_TAG = 0x7bb52d7a9fef58323eb1bf7a407db382d2f3f2d81bb1224f49fe518f6d48d37c;

    /// BIP-340 verify of the 32-byte `message` under the x-only key `px`.
    function verify(uint256 px, bytes32 message, bytes calldata sig) internal view returns (bool) {
        if (sig.length != 64) return false;
        uint256 rx = uint256(bytes32(sig[0:32]));
        uint256 s = uint256(bytes32(sig[32:64]));
        if (px >= P || rx >= P || s >= N) return false;
        uint256 e = uint256(sha256(abi.encodePacked(CHALLENGE_TAG, CHALLENGE_TAG, rx, px, message))) % N;
        address got = ecrecover(bytes32(mulmod(N - s, px, N)), 27, bytes32(px), bytes32(mulmod(N - e, px, N)));
        if (got == address(0)) return false;
        (bool ok, uint256 ry) = liftX(rx);
        return ok && got == address(uint160(uint256(keccak256(abi.encodePacked(rx, ry)))));
    }

    /// The even-y point with x-coordinate `x < p`; `ok` false if `x³ + 7` is not a square (or MODEXP failed).
    function liftX(uint256 x) internal view returns (bool ok, uint256 y) {
        uint256 rhs = addmod(mulmod(mulmod(x, x, P), x, P), 7, P);
        (ok, y) = _sqrtCandidate(rhs);
        if (!ok || mulmod(y, y, P) != rhs) return (false, 0);
        if (y & 1 == 1) y = P - y;
    }

    /// `a^((p+1)/4) mod p` via MODEXP (0x05); false if the call fails (out of gas), never reverts.
    function _sqrtCandidate(uint256 a) private view returns (bool ok, uint256 r) {
        assembly ("memory-safe") {
            let m := mload(0x40)
            mstore(m, 32)
            mstore(add(m, 0x20), 32)
            mstore(add(m, 0x40), 32)
            mstore(add(m, 0x60), a)
            mstore(add(m, 0x80), 0x3fffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffbfffff0c) // (p + 1) / 4
            mstore(add(m, 0xa0), P)
            let success := staticcall(gas(), 0x05, m, 0xc0, m, 0x20)
            ok := and(success, eq(returndatasize(), 0x20))
            r := mload(m)
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: musig2-secp256k1.** Create `smartcontracts/evm/src/ua/verifiers/Musig2Secp256k1Verifier.sol` with exactly this content:

```solidity
// SPDX-License-Identifier: LicenseRef-SCaaS-Community-1.0
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import {IUAPopVerifier} from "../IUAVerifier.sol";
import {Bip340} from "./Bip340.sol";

// sha256("KeyAgg list") and sha256("KeyAgg coefficient"): the BIP-327 KeyAgg tags (pinned by the tests).
bytes32 constant KEYAGG_LIST_TAG = 0x481c971c3c0b46d7f0b275ae598d4e2c7ed7319c594a5c6ec79ea0d4990294f0;
bytes32 constant KEYAGG_COEF_TAG = 0xbfc904034d1c88e8c80e22e53d24566d64824ed6427281c09100f94dcd52c981;

/// @title UA verifier for `musig2-secp256k1` (0x0110, accounts.md §1.6.1, §1.9).
/// @notice `verify(vk, m, sig)`: `vk = Q` (33, SEC1 compressed, from the registry's `keyPtr`), BIP-340 verify of the
///         32-byte `ua_msg` by `x(Q)` (BIP-327 output is BIP-340-valid; Q's parity plays no role here).
///         `verifyPop(keyData, m, pop)`: `keyData = Q (33) ‖ u16 n ‖ n × pk_i (33)`, `pop = sig (64) ‖ n × H_i (64)`.
///         Recomputes BIP-327 KeyAgg: `L = hash_{KeyAgg list}(pk_1 ‖ … ‖ pk_n)`, `a_i = hash_{KeyAgg coefficient}(L ‖
///         pk_i) mod N`, `a_i = 1` for the second distinct key (keys are strictly increasing, so it is `pk_2`); each hint
///         is checked as `address(H_i) == ecrecover(0, 25 + prefix_i, x(P_i), a_i·x(P_i) mod N)` (= `address(a_i·P_i)`,
///         `x(P_i) < N` required); the hints are summed in Jacobian coordinates (no inversion: `Q` is lifted with one
///         MODEXP and compared projectively, x and parity); then BIP-340 by `x(Q)` over `m`. A degenerate partial sum
///         (`S == ±H_i`, never met by an honest group) gives `Z = 0` and fails closed. Malformed input returns false,
///         never reverts. **UNAUDITED — NOT FOR PRODUCTION FUNDS.**
contract Musig2Secp256k1Verifier is IUAPopVerifier {
    uint256 private constant _P = Bip340.P;
    uint256 private constant _N = Bip340.N;

    function verify(bytes calldata vk, bytes32 message, bytes calldata sig) public view returns (bool) {
        return vk.length == 33 && (vk[0] == 0x02 || vk[0] == 0x03)
            && Bip340.verify(uint256(bytes32(vk[1:33])), message, sig);
    }

    function verifyPop(bytes calldata keyData, bytes32 message, bytes calldata pop) external view returns (bool) {
        if (keyData.length < 35) return false;
        uint256 n = uint16(bytes2(keyData[33:35]));
        if (n < 2 || n > 16 || keyData.length != 35 + 33 * n || pop.length != 64 + 64 * n) return false;
        return keyAgg(keyData, n, pop[64:]) && verify(keyData[0:33], message, pop[0:64]);
    }

    /// `Q` (keyData[0:33]) == Σ a_i·P_i with the hints `h = n × (x ‖ y)`; lengths checked by the caller.
    function keyAgg(bytes calldata keyData, uint256 n, bytes calldata h) internal view returns (bool) {
        bytes32 l = sha256(abi.encodePacked(KEYAGG_LIST_TAG, KEYAGG_LIST_TAG, keyData[35:]));
        (uint256 x1, uint256 y1, uint256 z1) = (0, 0, 0);
        for (uint256 i; i < n; ++i) {
            (bool ok, uint256 hx, uint256 hy) = _hint(keyData, h, l, i);
            if (!ok) return false;
            if (i == 0) (x1, y1, z1) = (hx, hy, 1);
            else (x1, y1, z1) = _addMixed(x1, y1, z1, hx, hy);
        }
        // Q from its compressed form: x(Q) < p, on the curve, y of the stated parity; compare X == x·Z², Y == y·Z³
        uint256 qx = uint256(bytes32(keyData[1:33]));
        if (qx >= _P || z1 == 0) return false;
        (bool ok, uint256 qy) = Bip340.liftX(qx);
        if (!ok) return false;
        if (keyData[0] == 0x03) qy = _P - qy;
        uint256 zz = mulmod(z1, z1, _P);
        return x1 == mulmod(qx, zz, _P) && y1 == mulmod(qy, mulmod(zz, z1, _P), _P);
    }

    /// Key `i`: SEC1 prefix, `x(P_i) < N`, strictly above key `i − 1` (KeySort), and the hint `H_i == a_i·P_i` by the
    /// `ecrecover` trick; returns `H_i`.
    function _hint(bytes calldata keyData, bytes calldata h, bytes32 l, uint256 i)
        private
        view
        returns (bool, uint256 hx, uint256 hy)
    {
        uint256 o = 35 + 33 * i;
        uint8 prefix = uint8(keyData[o]);
        uint256 x = uint256(bytes32(keyData[o + 1:o + 33]));
        if ((prefix != 2 && prefix != 3) || x >= _N) return (false, 0, 0);
        if (i > 0) {
            uint8 pp = uint8(keyData[o - 33]);
            if (pp > prefix || (pp == prefix && uint256(bytes32(keyData[o - 32:o])) >= x)) return (false, 0, 0);
        }
        // a_i = 1 for the second distinct key (BIP-327 GetSecondKey; keys are distinct and sorted, so it is pk_2)
        uint256 a = i == 1 ? 1 : _coef(l, keyData[o:o + 33]);
        (hx, hy) = (uint256(bytes32(h[64 * i:64 * i + 32])), uint256(bytes32(h[64 * i + 32:64 * i + 64])));
        if (hx >= _P || hy >= _P) return (false, 0, 0);
        address got = ecrecover(0, 25 + prefix, bytes32(x), bytes32(mulmod(a, x, _N)));
        return (got != address(0) && got == address(uint160(uint256(keccak256(abi.encodePacked(hx, hy))))), hx, hy);
    }

    /// `a_i = hash_{KeyAgg coefficient}(L ‖ pk_i) mod N`.
    function _coef(bytes32 l, bytes calldata pk) private view returns (uint256) {
        return uint256(sha256(abi.encodePacked(KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, l, pk))) % _N;
    }

    /// Jacobian (x1, y1, z1) + affine (x2, y2) on y² = x³ + 7 (textbook mixed addition). `H == 0` (doubling or
    /// P + (−P)) yields z3 = 0, which every later step keeps at 0: the caller then refuses.
    function _addMixed(uint256 x1, uint256 y1, uint256 z1, uint256 x2, uint256 y2)
        private
        pure
        returns (uint256 x3, uint256 y3, uint256 z3)
    {
        uint256 zz = mulmod(z1, z1, _P);
        uint256 hh = addmod(mulmod(x2, zz, _P), _P - x1, _P); // U2 − X1
        uint256 r = addmod(mulmod(y2, mulmod(zz, z1, _P), _P), _P - y1, _P); // S2 − Y1
        uint256 h2 = mulmod(hh, hh, _P);
        uint256 h3 = mulmod(h2, hh, _P);
        uint256 v = mulmod(x1, h2, _P);
        x3 = addmod(addmod(mulmod(r, r, _P), _P - h3, _P), _P - addmod(v, v, _P), _P);
        y3 = addmod(mulmod(r, addmod(v, _P - x3, _P), _P), _P - mulmod(y1, h3, _P), _P);
        z3 = mulmod(z1, hh, _P);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: musig2-bn254.** Create `smartcontracts/evm/src/ua/verifiers/Musig2Bn254Verifier.sol` with exactly this content:

```solidity
// SPDX-License-Identifier: LicenseRef-SCaaS-Community-1.0
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import {CurveSigs} from "../../common/CurveSigs.sol";
import {G1Bn254} from "../../zether/ZetherCurves.sol";
import {UASafeVerifier, SCHNORR_TAG} from "./PairingVerifiers.sol";
import {KEYAGG_LIST_TAG, KEYAGG_COEF_TAG} from "./Musig2Secp256k1Verifier.sol";

/// @title UA verifier for `musig2-bn254` (0x0112, accounts.md §1.6.1, §1.9).
/// @notice `verify(vk, m, sig)`: `vk = Q` (64, from `keyPtr`), the scaas Schnorr of `schnorr-bn254` (`R ‖ z`, 96).
///         `verifyPop(keyData, m, sig)`: `keyData = Q (64) ‖ u16 n ‖ n × P_i (64)`, `P_i` strictly increasing; the
///         BIP-327 KeyAgg port: `L = hash_{KeyAgg list}(P_1 ‖ … ‖ P_n)` (BIP-327's 32-byte tagged SHA-256),
///         `a_i = H_{KeyAgg coefficient}(L ‖ P_i)` with the widened hash `H_t(x) = tagged_t(x ‖ 0x00) ‖ tagged_t(x ‖ 0x01)`
///         read as a 512-bit integer mod r, `a_i = 1` for the second key (`P_2`), `Q == Σ a_i·P_i` (ECMUL/ECADD, full
///         points, no parity), then the Schnorr by `Q`. Runs inside `UASafeVerifier` (an invalid point makes the
///         precompile fail and `G1Bn254` revert: false). **UNAUDITED — NOT FOR PRODUCTION FUNDS.**
contract Musig2Bn254Verifier is UASafeVerifier {
    /// verifyPop at n = 16 measured 188,045 inside `check` (`Musig2Verifiers.t.sol` `test_bnPopGas`); ~20 % margin.
    uint256 public constant POP_GAS = 230_000;

    /// verify = `schnorr-bn254`: valid ≈ 27 k inside `check`.
    constructor() UASafeVerifier(40_000) {}

    function _checkGas(bool pop) internal view override returns (uint256) {
        return pop ? POP_GAS : CHECK_GAS;
    }

    function check(bytes calldata keyData, bytes32 message, bytes calldata sig, bool pop)
        external
        view
        override
        returns (bool)
    {
        if (!pop) return keyData.length == 64 && CurveSigs.schnorr(SCHNORR_TAG, keyData, message, sig);
        return keyAgg(keyData) && CurveSigs.schnorr(SCHNORR_TAG, keyData[0:64], message, sig);
    }

    function keyAgg(bytes calldata d) internal view returns (bool) {
        if (d.length < 66) return false;
        uint256 n = uint16(bytes2(d[64:66]));
        if (n < 2 || n > 16 || d.length != 66 + 64 * n) return false;
        bytes32 l = sha256(abi.encodePacked(KEYAGG_LIST_TAG, KEYAGG_LIST_TAG, d[66:]));
        uint256[] memory sc = new uint256[](n);
        uint256 twoTo256 = (type(uint256).max % G1Bn254.R) + 1;
        for (uint256 i; i < n; ++i) {
            uint256 o = 66 + 64 * i;
            if (i > 0) {
                (uint256 px, uint256 x) = (uint256(bytes32(d[o - 64:o - 32])), uint256(bytes32(d[o:o + 32])));
                if (px > x || (px == x && uint256(bytes32(d[o - 32:o])) >= uint256(bytes32(d[o + 32:o + 64])))) {
                    return false; // strictly increasing
                }
            }
            if (i == 1) {
                sc[i] = 1;
                continue;
            }
            uint256 hi = uint256(sha256(abi.encodePacked(KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, l, d[o:o + 64], uint8(0))));
            uint256 lo = uint256(sha256(abi.encodePacked(KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, l, d[o:o + 64], uint8(1))));
            sc[i] = addmod(mulmod(hi, twoTo256, G1Bn254.R), lo, G1Bn254.R);
        }
        return keccak256(G1Bn254.msm(d[66:], sc)) == keccak256(d[0:64]);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Group fixtures (test helper).** Create `smartcontracts/evm/test/ua/GroupFixtures.sol` with exactly this content:

```solidity
// SPDX-License-Identifier: LicenseRef-SCaaS-Community-1.0
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import {Test, Vm} from "forge-std/Test.sol";
import {G1Bn254} from "../../src/zether/ZetherCurves.sol";
import {ZetherTranscript} from "../../src/zether/ZetherTranscript.sol";
import {FrostSecp256k1} from "../../src/ua/verifiers/UAFrostSecp256k1Verifier.sol";

/// Runs a call in its own small frame and returns the gas it used: a gasleft() delta in the test itself would include
/// the expansion of the test's large memory (parsed JSON) while encoding the call.
contract GasMeter {
    function measure(address target, bytes calldata data) external returns (bool ok, bytes memory ret, uint256 used) {
        uint256 g = gasleft();
        (ok, ret) = target.call(data);
        used = g - gasleft();
    }
}

/// The UA-2c groups of `vectors/ua/groups.json` (Rust signers: BIP-327 MuSig2, ZF frost-secp256k1 3.0.0 and the bn254
/// ports; PoPs under structures.json context A = this suite's `REG_A` on chain 31337) and signers over the group secret
/// for messages the file does not carry: BIP-340 by `x(Q)` (musig2-secp256k1), RFC 9591 FROST(secp256k1, SHA-256)
/// (frost-secp256k1), the scaas Schnorr (both bn254 kinds). An aggregate MuSig2 / FROST signature has the same form as
/// the single-signer one by the group secret.
abstract contract GroupFixtures is Test {
    uint256 internal constant N_K1 = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEBAAEDCE6AF48A03BBFD25E8CD0364141;
    uint16 internal constant MS = 0x0110;
    uint16 internal constant FS = 0x0111;
    uint16 internal constant MB = 0x0112;
    uint16 internal constant FB = 0x0113;

    GasMeter internal meter = new GasMeter();

    struct Group {
        string row; // ".groups[i]"
        uint16 kind;
        uint256 n;
        uint256 t;
        bytes keyData;
        bytes vk;
        bytes hints; // musig2-secp256k1: n × (x ‖ y), concatenated
        uint256 secret; // groupSecret: MuSig2 d = Σ a_i·sk_i, FROST s = Σ a_i0
        bytes32 ua;
        bytes32 keyHash;
        bytes32 popMessage;
        bytes pop; // pop.blob, context A
    }

    function _gj() internal view returns (string memory) {
        return vm.readFile("../vectors/ua/groups.json");
    }

    /// The group of `kind` with `n` members / participants and threshold `t` (MuSig2: t = n).
    function _group(uint16 kind, uint256 n, uint256 t) internal view returns (Group memory g) {
        string memory j = _gj();
        for (uint256 i; vm.keyExistsJson(j, string.concat(".groups[", vm.toString(i), "]")); ++i) {
            string memory row = string.concat(".groups[", vm.toString(i), "]");
            if (
                vm.parseJsonUint(j, string.concat(row, ".kind")) == kind
                    && vm.parseJsonUint(j, string.concat(row, ".n")) == n
                    && vm.parseJsonUint(j, string.concat(row, ".t")) == t
            ) return _load(j, row);
        }
        revert("group not in groups.json");
    }

    function _load(string memory j, string memory row) internal pure returns (Group memory g) {
        g.row = row;
        g.kind = uint16(vm.parseJsonUint(j, string.concat(row, ".kind")));
        g.n = vm.parseJsonUint(j, string.concat(row, ".n"));
        g.t = vm.parseJsonUint(j, string.concat(row, ".t"));
        g.keyData = vm.parseJsonBytes(j, string.concat(row, ".keyData"));
        g.vk = vm.parseJsonBytes(j, string.concat(row, ".vk"));
        bytes[] memory h = vm.parseJsonBytesArray(j, string.concat(row, ".hints"));
        for (uint256 i; i < h.length; ++i) {
            g.hints = bytes.concat(g.hints, h[i]);
        }
        g.secret = vm.parseJsonUint(j, string.concat(row, ".groupSecret"));
        g.ua = vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat(row, ".ua"));
        g.keyHash = vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat(row, ".keyHash"));
        g.popMessage = vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat(row, ".pop.message"));
        g.pop = vm.parseJsonBytes(j, string.concat(row, ".pop.blob"));
    }

    /// The `main` group of `kind`: MuSig2 n = 3, FROST t = 2, n = 3.
    function _main(uint16 kind) internal view returns (Group memory) {
        return _group(kind, 3, kind == MS || kind == MB ? 3 : 2);
    }

    // ----- signers over the group secret -----

    /// BIP-340 signature of `m` by the secret `d` (any parity; the signer negates as BIP-340 says).
    function _bip340Sign(uint256 d, bytes32 m) internal returns (bytes memory) {
        Vm.Wallet memory w = vm.createWallet(d);
        if (w.publicKeyY & 1 == 1) d = N_K1 - d;
        uint256 k = uint256(keccak256(abi.encode("nonce", d, m))) % (N_K1 - 1) + 1;
        Vm.Wallet memory wr = vm.createWallet(k);
        if (wr.publicKeyY & 1 == 1) k = N_K1 - k;
        bytes32 t = sha256("BIP0340/challenge");
        uint256 e = uint256(sha256(abi.encodePacked(t, t, wr.publicKeyX, w.publicKeyX, m))) % N_K1;
        return abi.encodePacked(wr.publicKeyX, addmod(k, mulmod(e, d, N_K1), N_K1));
    }

    /// RFC 9591 FROST(secp256k1, SHA-256) by the group secret: `R ‖ z`, `c = H2(R ‖ Y ‖ m)`, `z = k + c·s`.
    function _frostSign(uint256 s, bytes memory y, bytes32 m) internal returns (bytes memory) {
        uint256 k = uint256(keccak256(abi.encode("frost-nonce", s, m))) % (N_K1 - 1) + 1;
        Vm.Wallet memory w = vm.createWallet(k);
        bytes memory r = abi.encodePacked(bytes1(uint8(2 + (w.publicKeyY & 1))), w.publicKeyX);
        uint256 c = FrostSecp256k1.hashToScalar(abi.encodePacked(r, y, m), FrostSecp256k1.DST_CHAL);
        return bytes.concat(r, bytes32(addmod(k, mulmod(c, s, N_K1), N_K1)));
    }

    /// scaas Schnorr on bn254 (`schnorr-bn254`): `R ‖ z`, `e = hashToScalar(keccak256(tag ‖ R ‖ Y ‖ m))`.
    function _bnSign(uint256 x, bytes memory y, bytes32 m) internal view returns (bytes memory) {
        uint256 k = uint256(keccak256(abi.encode("nonce", x, m))) % (G1Bn254.R - 1) + 1;
        bytes memory r = G1Bn254.mulBase(k);
        uint256 e =
            ZetherTranscript.hashToScalar(keccak256(abi.encodePacked("scaas.ua.schnorr.v1", r, y, m)), G1Bn254.R);
        return bytes.concat(r, bytes32(addmod(k, mulmod(e, x, G1Bn254.R), G1Bn254.R)));
    }

    /// The group signature of `kind` over `m` by `secret` for the key `vk` (no hints).
    function _gsign(uint16 kind, uint256 secret, bytes memory vk, bytes32 m) internal returns (bytes memory) {
        if (kind == MS) return _bip340Sign(secret, m);
        if (kind == FS) return _frostSign(secret, vk, m);
        return _bnSign(secret, vk, m);
    }

    /// `target.call(data)` measured by `meter`; true iff it returned exactly `abi.encode(true)`.
    function _metered(address target, bytes memory data) internal returns (bool, uint256 used) {
        (bool ok, bytes memory ret, uint256 g) = meter.measure(target, data);
        return (ok && ret.length == 32 && abi.decode(ret, (bool)), g);
    }

    // ----- bytes -----

    function _head(bytes memory d, uint256 len) internal pure returns (bytes memory) {
        return _slice(d, 0, len);
    }

    function _slice(bytes memory b, uint256 o, uint256 len) internal pure returns (bytes memory c) {
        c = new bytes(len);
        for (uint256 i; i < len; ++i) {
            c[i] = b[o + i];
        }
    }

    function _flip(bytes memory b, uint256 i) internal pure returns (bytes memory c) {
        c = bytes.concat(b);
        c[i] = c[i] ^ 0x01;
    }

    /// Swaps the `size`-byte members at offsets `a` and `b`.
    function _swap(bytes memory d, uint256 a, uint256 b, uint256 size) internal pure returns (bytes memory c) {
        c = bytes.concat(d);
        for (uint256 i; i < size; ++i) {
            (c[a + i], c[b + i]) = (d[b + i], d[a + i]);
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Verifier tests.** Create `smartcontracts/evm/test/ua/verifiers/Musig2Verifiers.t.sol` with exactly this content:

```solidity
// SPDX-License-Identifier: LicenseRef-SCaaS-Community-1.0
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import {Vm} from "forge-std/Vm.sol";
import {
    Musig2Secp256k1Verifier,
    KEYAGG_LIST_TAG,
    KEYAGG_COEF_TAG
} from "../../../src/ua/verifiers/Musig2Secp256k1Verifier.sol";
import {Musig2Bn254Verifier} from "../../../src/ua/verifiers/Musig2Bn254Verifier.sol";
import {Bip340} from "../../../src/ua/verifiers/Bip340.sol";
import {G1Bn254} from "../../../src/zether/ZetherCurves.sol";
import {GroupFixtures} from "../GroupFixtures.sol";

/// UA-2c MuSig2 verifiers (accounts.md §1.6.1, §1.9) on groups.json (BIP-327 signer in Rust; n = 2, 3, 8, 16): KeyAgg
/// with hints (secp256k1) and with ECMUL/ECADD (bn254), the group signature against the stored `Q`, every groups.json
/// negative, and the negatives the file does not carry — the second-key rule, `x(P_i) ≥ N`, flipped hints, malformed
/// lengths (false, never a revert). Gas logged with -vv.
contract Musig2VerifiersTest is GroupFixtures {
    Musig2Secp256k1Verifier internal secp;
    Musig2Bn254Verifier internal bn;
    bytes32 internal constant M = keccak256("scaas.ua.test.musig2");
    /// 02 ‖ N: a valid secp256k1 point (N³ + 7 is a square mod p) with x ≥ N, which the ecrecover trick cannot take.
    bytes internal constant KEY_X_N = hex"02fffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffebaaedce6af48a03bbfd25e8cd0364141";

    function setUp() public {
        secp = new Musig2Secp256k1Verifier();
        bn = new Musig2Bn254Verifier();
    }

    function test_tags() public pure {
        assertEq(sha256("KeyAgg list"), KEYAGG_LIST_TAG);
        assertEq(sha256("KeyAgg coefficient"), KEYAGG_COEF_TAG);
        assertEq(sha256("BIP0340/challenge"), Bip340.CHALLENGE_TAG);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ groups.json

    function test_groups() public {
        uint256[4] memory ns = [uint256(2), 3, 8, 16];
        for (uint256 i; i < ns.length; ++i) {
            _groupRow(_group(MS, ns[i], ns[i]), address(secp), "musig2-secp256k1", 64);
            _groupRow(_group(MB, ns[i], ns[i]), address(bn), "musig2-bn254", 96);
        }
    }

    function _groupRow(Group memory g, address v, string memory name, uint256 sigLen) internal {
        string memory label = string.concat(name, " n=", vm.toString(g.n));
        (bool ok, uint256 used) = _metered(v, abi.encodeCall(secp.verifyPop, (g.keyData, g.popMessage, g.pop)));
        assertTrue(ok, label);
        emit log_named_uint(string.concat(label, " verifyPop"), used);
        bytes memory sig = _head(g.pop, sigLen);
        (ok, used) = _metered(v, abi.encodeCall(secp.verify, (g.vk, g.popMessage, sig)));
        assertTrue(ok, label);
        emit log_named_uint(string.concat(label, " verify"), used);
        (ok,) = _metered(v, abi.encodeCall(secp.verifyPop, (g.keyData, ~g.popMessage, g.pop)));
        assertFalse(ok, "pop over another message");
        bytes memory own = _gsign(g.kind, g.secret, g.vk, M);
        (ok,) = _metered(v, abi.encodeCall(secp.verify, (g.vk, M, own)));
        assertTrue(ok, "a signature by the group secret");
    }

    /// The main groups' `sigs` rows (metatx, member) verify against `vk`.
    function test_mainSigs() public view {
        string memory j = _gj();
        Group[2] memory gs = [_main(MS), _main(MB)];
        for (uint256 k; k < 2; ++k) {
            for (uint256 i; i < 2; ++i) {
                string memory r = string.concat(gs[k].row, ".sigs[", vm.toString(i), "]");
                bytes32 m = vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat(r, ".message"));
                bytes memory sig = vm.parseJsonBytes(j, string.concat(r, ".sig"));
                assertTrue(k == 0 ? secp.verify(gs[k].vk, m, sig) : bn.verify(gs[k].vk, m, sig));
            }
        }
    }

    /// Every groups.json negative of the MuSig2 kinds: `register` rows fail `verifyPop` (incl. those the registry
    /// already refuses as UAKeyData), `verify` rows fail `verify`; a `sigValid` row's signature alone verifies.
    function test_vectorNegatives() public view {
        string memory j = _gj();
        uint16[2] memory kinds = [MS, MB];
        uint256 seen;
        for (uint256 k; k < 2; ++k) {
            string memory row = _main(kinds[k]).row;
            for (uint256 i; vm.keyExistsJson(j, string.concat(row, ".negatives[", vm.toString(i), "]")); ++i) {
                _vectorNegative(j, kinds[k], string.concat(row, ".negatives[", vm.toString(i), "]"));
                ++seen;
            }
        }
        assertEq(seen, 27); // 15 + 12
    }

    function _vectorNegative(string memory j, uint16 kind, string memory r) internal view {
        (uint256 vkLen, uint256 sigLen) = kind == MS ? (33, 64) : (64, 96);
        string memory name = vm.parseJsonString(j, string.concat(r, ".name"));
        bytes32 m = vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat(r, ".message"));
        if (keccak256(bytes(vm.parseJsonString(j, string.concat(r, ".entry")))) == keccak256("verify")) {
            bytes memory vk = vm.parseJsonBytes(j, string.concat(r, ".vk"));
            bytes memory s = vm.parseJsonBytes(j, string.concat(r, ".sig"));
            assertFalse(kind == MS ? secp.verify(vk, m, s) : bn.verify(vk, m, s), name);
            return;
        }
        bytes memory d = vm.parseJsonBytes(j, string.concat(r, ".keyData"));
        bytes memory pop = vm.parseJsonBytes(j, string.concat(r, ".pop"));
        assertFalse(kind == MS ? secp.verifyPop(d, m, pop) : bn.verifyPop(d, m, pop), name);
        if (vm.parseJsonBool(j, string.concat(r, ".sigValid"))) {
            bytes memory s = _head(pop, sigLen);
            assertTrue(kind == MS ? secp.verify(_head(d, vkLen), m, s) : bn.verify(_head(d, vkLen), m, s), name);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ own negatives (secp256k1)

    /// BIP-327's `a = 1` for the second key: the same list aggregated with a hashed `a_2` is a consistent group (Q',
    /// hints, d') that a verifier hashing every coefficient would accept. The test's coefficient formula is first pinned
    /// against groups.json `coefficients[0]`.
    function test_secpSecondKeyRule() public {
        (bytes memory kd, bytes memory hints, uint256 d) = _secpWithoutSecondKeyRule(_main(MS));
        bytes memory sig = _bip340Sign(d, M);
        assertTrue(secp.verify(_head(kd, 33), M, sig), "its signature is fine");
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(kd, M, bytes.concat(sig, hints)));
    }

    /// The main group's key data, hints and secret with `a_2` hashed like every other coefficient.
    function _secpWithoutSecondKeyRule(Group memory g)
        internal
        returns (bytes memory kd, bytes memory hints, uint256 d)
    {
        (uint256 a2, uint256 sk2) = _hashedA2(g);
        d = addmod(g.secret, mulmod(a2 - 1, sk2, N_K1), N_K1);
        Vm.Wallet memory q = vm.createWallet(d);
        kd = bytes.concat(bytes1(uint8(2 + (q.publicKeyY & 1))), bytes32(q.publicKeyX), _slice(g.keyData, 33, 101));
        Vm.Wallet memory h2 = vm.createWallet(mulmod(a2, sk2, N_K1));
        hints =
            bytes.concat(_head(g.hints, 64), abi.encodePacked(h2.publicKeyX, h2.publicKeyY), _slice(g.hints, 128, 64));
    }

    /// `hash_{KeyAgg coefficient}(L ‖ pk_2) mod N` and `sk_2`; the formula pinned on `coefficients[0]` first.
    function _hashedA2(Group memory g) internal view returns (uint256, uint256) {
        string memory j = _gj();
        bytes[] memory pks = vm.parseJsonBytesArray(j, string.concat(g.row, ".members"));
        uint256[] memory a = vm.parseJsonUintArray(j, string.concat(g.row, ".coefficients"));
        bytes32 l = vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat(g.row, ".L"));
        assertEq(a[1], 1, "a_2 = 1");
        assertEq(_coef(l, pks[0]), a[0], "coefficient formula");
        return (_coef(l, pks[1]), vm.parseJsonUintArray(j, string.concat(g.row, ".secrets"))[1]);
    }

    function _coef(bytes32 l, bytes memory pk) internal pure returns (uint256) {
        return uint256(sha256(abi.encodePacked(KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, l, pk))) % N_K1;
    }

    /// `x(P_i) ≥ N` (accounts.md §1.6.1: required `< N`); the registry test shows the point passes key validation.
    function test_secpKeyXAtLeastN() public {
        Group memory g = _group(MS, 2, 2);
        bytes memory other = _slice(g.keyData, 35, 33);
        bytes memory d = other[0] == bytes1(0x03)
            ? bytes.concat(g.vk, hex"0002", KEY_X_N, other)
            : bytes.concat(g.vk, hex"0002", other, KEY_X_N);
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(d, M, bytes.concat(_bip340Sign(g.secret, M), g.hints)));
    }

    function test_secpWrongHint() public view {
        Group memory g = _main(MS);
        bytes memory sig = _head(g.pop, 64);
        assertTrue(secp.verifyPop(g.keyData, g.popMessage, g.pop));
        for (uint256 i; i < 3; ++i) {
            assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(g.keyData, g.popMessage, bytes.concat(sig, _flip(g.hints, 64 * i + 63))), "y");
            assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(g.keyData, g.popMessage, bytes.concat(sig, _flip(g.hints, 64 * i + 5))), "x");
        }
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(g.keyData, g.popMessage, bytes.concat(sig, _swap(g.hints, 0, 128, 64))), "swapped");
    }

    function test_secpMalformed() public view {
        Group memory g = _main(MS);
        (bytes memory kd, bytes memory pop, bytes32 m) = (g.keyData, g.pop, g.popMessage);
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(_head(kd, kd.length - 1), m, pop), "key data - 1");
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(bytes.concat(kd, hex"00"), m, pop), "key data + 1");
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop("", m, pop), "empty key data");
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(kd, m, ""), "empty pop");
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(kd, m, bytes.concat(pop, hex"00")), "pop + 1");
        bytes memory d = bytes.concat(kd);
        d[34] = 0x02; // n = 2 with three keys
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(d, m, pop), "n mismatch");
        d = bytes.concat(kd);
        d[35] = 0x04; // prefix of key 0
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(d, m, pop), "member prefix 04");
        d = bytes.concat(kd);
        for (uint256 b = 1; b < 33; ++b) {
            d[b] = 0;
        }
        d[32] = 0x05; // x(Q) = 5: off the curve
        assertFalse(secp.verifyPop(d, m, pop), "Q off curve");
        bytes memory sig = _head(pop, 64);
        assertFalse(secp.verify(_slice(g.vk, 1, 32), m, sig), "x-only vk");
        bytes memory q4 = bytes.concat(g.vk);
        q4[0] = 0x04;
        assertFalse(secp.verify(q4, m, sig), "vk prefix 04");
        assertFalse(secp.verify(g.vk, m, bytes.concat(sig, hex"00")), "sig 65");
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ own negatives (bn254)

    /// The bn254 port's second-key rule (a_2 = 1), as for secp256k1, with the widened coefficient hash.
    function test_bnSecondKeyRule() public view {
        Group memory g = _main(MB);
        string memory j = _gj();
        bytes[] memory pks = vm.parseJsonBytesArray(j, string.concat(g.row, ".members"));
        uint256[] memory sks = vm.parseJsonUintArray(j, string.concat(g.row, ".secrets"));
        uint256[] memory a = vm.parseJsonUintArray(j, string.concat(g.row, ".coefficients"));
        bytes32 l = vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat(g.row, ".L"));
        assertEq(a[1], 1, "a_2 = 1");
        assertEq(_wide(l, pks[0]), a[0], "coefficient formula");
        uint256 d = addmod(g.secret, mulmod(_wide(l, pks[1]) - 1, sks[1], G1Bn254.R), G1Bn254.R);
        bytes memory q = G1Bn254.mulBase(d);
        bytes memory kd = bytes.concat(q, _slice(g.keyData, 64, g.keyData.length - 64));
        bytes memory sig = _bnSign(d, q, M);
        assertTrue(bn.verify(q, M, sig), "its signature is fine");
        assertFalse(bn.verifyPop(kd, M, sig));
    }

    function _wide(bytes32 l, bytes memory pk) internal pure returns (uint256) {
        uint256 hi = uint256(sha256(abi.encodePacked(KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, l, pk, uint8(0))));
        uint256 lo = uint256(sha256(abi.encodePacked(KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, KEYAGG_COEF_TAG, l, pk, uint8(1))));
        return addmod(mulmod(hi, (type(uint256).max % G1Bn254.R) + 1, G1Bn254.R), lo, G1Bn254.R);
    }

    /// Invalid points make the precompile fail inside the bounded self-call: false, never a revert.
    function test_bnMalformed() public view {
        Group memory g = _main(MB);
        (bytes memory kd, bytes memory sig, bytes32 m) = (g.keyData, g.pop, g.popMessage);
        assertTrue(bn.verifyPop(kd, m, sig));
        assertFalse(bn.verifyPop(_flip(kd, 66 + 64 + 63), m, sig), "member off curve");
        assertFalse(bn.verifyPop(_flip(kd, 63), m, sig), "Q off curve");
        assertFalse(bn.verifyPop(_head(kd, kd.length - 1), m, sig), "key data - 1");
        bytes memory d = bytes.concat(kd);
        d[65] = 0x02;
        assertFalse(bn.verifyPop(d, m, sig), "n mismatch");
        assertFalse(bn.verifyPop("", m, sig), "empty");
        assertFalse(bn.verify(kd, m, sig), "verify takes Q only");
    }

    /// verifyPop's worst case (n = 16) fits `POP_GAS` inside `check`.
    function test_bnPopGas() public {
        Group memory g = _group(MB, 16, 16);
        (bool ok, uint256 used) =
            _metered(address(bn), abi.encodeCall(bn.check, (g.keyData, g.popMessage, g.pop, true)));
        assertTrue(ok);
        emit log_named_uint("musig2-bn254 check(pop) n=16", used);
        assertLt(used, bn.POP_GAS());
    }
}
```
